/*   parse - parser.
   84/06/17. A. E. Klietz.
   Copyright (c) 1984.  Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
struct pa_parmlist {
 char *pa_pname;
 word pa_ptype;
};
getword(wrd, numptr)
/* Return next   "string#num" sequence from the input buffer. */
char *wrd;
word *numptr;
{
 char buf[BUFLEN];
 sparse(buffer, wrd);
 *numptr = 0;
 if (parseloc >= 0 && buffer[parseloc-1] == '#') {
  sparse(buffer, buf);
  *numptr = atoi(buf);
 }
 else
  if ((*numptr = atoi(wrd)) != 0)
   /* its a number not a string */
   wrd[0] = '\0';
}
 
word geteditparm(parmlist, worde, data)
/* Return index of given parameter and it's data. */
/*    parameter=data */
struct pa_parmlist parmlist[];
char *worde;
word *data;
{
 char buf[BUFLEN];
 register char ch;
 register i;
 worde[0] = '\0';
 *data = 0;
 if (parseloc < 0)
  return(0);
 buf[0] = '\0';
 for (i=0; i < BUFLEN-1; ++i) {
  if ((ch = buffer[parseloc++]) == '=')
   break;
  if ((buf[i] = ch) == '\0')
   break;
 }
  
 if (i == 1)
  buf[i++] = ' ';
 buf[i] = '\0';
 string_tolower(buf);
  
 if (ch == '=') {
  for (i=0; parmlist[i].pa_pname != NULL; ++i)
   if (subset(parmlist[i].pa_pname, buf))
    break;
  if (parmlist[i].pa_pname == NULL) {
   mwrite(My_mask, "Unknown parameter - %s.\n", buf);  
   return(0);
  }
 }
 else {
  parseloc = -1;
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1221));
  /* Missing parameter.\n */
  return(0);
 }
 if (parmlist[i].pa_ptype == DOTHER)
  return(i);
 if (sparse(buffer, worde) <= 0) {
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1221));
  return(0);
 }
 switch (parmlist[i].pa_ptype) {
case DFLAG:
 if (worde[0] == 't')
  *data = TRUE;
 else
  if (worde[0] == 'f')
   *data = FALSE;
  else {
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(1222), buf);
   /* %s - must be TRUE or FALSE.\n */
   return(0);
  }
 break;
case DNUM:
 if ((*data = atoi(worde)) == 0 && worde[0] != '0') {
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1223), buf);
  /* %s - must be a number. */
  return(0);
 }
 break;
case DWORD:
 break;
 
 }
 
 return(i);   
}
sparse(buf, worde)
char *buf, *worde;
{
 register len;
 len = parse(buf, worde);
 if (len == 4 && (equal(worde, "from") || equal(worde, "with"))
  || len == 3 && equal(worde, "for")
  || len == 5 && equal(worde, "spell"))
   return(sparse(buf, worde));
 return(len);
}
LSE.\n */
   return(0);
  }
 break;
case DNUM:
 if ((*data = atoi(worde)) == 0 && worde[0] != '0') {
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1223), buf);
  /* %s - must be a number. */
  return(0);
 }
 break;
case DWORD:
 break;
 
 }

/*  promptuser - prompt the user
  84/06/04. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
extern boolean noprompt;
promptuser()
/* Issue a prompt based on the user->us_entry parameter */
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 usr = user;
 usr->us_encountstop = FALSE;
 switch (usr->us_entry) {
case XNEWCLASS: mwrite(My_mask,"Class"); 
    break;
case XSTATS: mwrite(My_mask, "Stats");
    break;
case XSKILL: mwrite(My_mask, "Skill");
    break;
case XSEX:   mwrite(My_mask, "%s", desc(500));
    /* Finally, is your character a (M)ale or a (F)emale */
    break;
case XCMD:
    if (usr->us_brief) 
     mwrite(My_mask, "Do");
    else
     mwrite(My_mask, "Action");
    break;
case XEDIT:
    if (usr->us_brief)
     mwrite(My_mask, "Ed");
    else
     mwrite(My_mask, "Edit command");
    break;
case XNOTICE:   mwrite(My_mask, "Enter notice");
    break;
case XDEAD:
{ 
  register i;
  for (i=501; i <= 505; ++i)
   mwrite(My_mask, fmt, desc(i));
    /* Congratulations!  Your death has made the newspapers! */
    /* In one line, try to explain what killed you and how.  Use */
    /* words like "He" and "She" instead of "I". */
    /* Example: "His weapon broke, and before he knew it the vampire */
    /* drained his life away.." */
   break;
}
case XSPELL:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Say chant");
 break;
case XNEWS:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Enter news");
 break;
case XSELL:
case XREPAIR:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Yes/no");
 break;
case XWISH:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Enter wish");
 break;
case XSURE:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Are you sure");
 break;
case XPARLEY:
 mwrite(My_mask, "Would you like to have it, Effiendi");
 break;
default:
 errlog(fmt, desc(506));
 /* sce506 - Unknown prompt. */
 break;
}
}

/*  desc - string file descriptions.
  84/06/08. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
static unsigned edescrp = 0;
static  char *textline;
static char text[256];
char *desc(index)
word index;
{
 if (edescrp == 0)
  if ((edescrp = random_open("/data/scepter/edescrp")) == 0)
   crash("Unable to open edescrp file.\n");
 if (random_read(edescrp, index, text, 256) == 0)
  text[0] = '\0';
 text[255] = '\0';
 return(text);
}
writedesc(index, line)
word index;
char *line;
{
 register i, j;
 j = strlen(line) + 1;
 for (i=0; i < j-1; ++i)
  if (line[i] == '\\' && line[i+1] == 'n') {
   line[i] = '\n';
   strcpy(&line[i+1], &line[i+2]);
   --j;
  }
 if (j <= 1)
  j = 0;
 
 if (random_write(edescrp, index, line, j) != j)
  crash("Unable to write line %d into edescrp file!\n", index);
}
char  *printdesc(line, phrase, brief)
word line, phrase;
boolean brief;
{
 char *bufptr;
 register char *buf;   
 register i, j;
 register len, phrasenum;

  for (i=0, phrase = 0; i < len; )
   if (buf[i++] == '/') 
    ++phrase;
  phrase = ran(phrase - 1);
 }
 for (i=0, phrasenum = 0; i < len; )
  if (buf[i++] == '/') {
   ++phrasenum;
   if (phrasenum == phrase)
    break;
  }
 if (i >= len) {
  tsprintf(text, "Bad phrase (#%d) for line #%d", phrase, line);
  free(buf);
  return(text);
 }
 if (textline != NULL)
  free(textline);
 textline = alloc(len);
 brmark = FALSE;
 for (j=0; i < len; ++i) {
  if (buf[i] == '/')
   break;
  
  if (buf[i] == '#') 
   brmark = !brmark;
  else {
   if (!brmark || !brief) {
    if (buf[i] == '\\' && buf[i+1] == 'n') {
     ++i;
     textline[j++] = '\n';
    }
    else
     textline[j++] = buf[i];
   }
  }
 }
 textline[j] = '\0';
 free(buf);
 return(textline);
}

/*  matchdelete.c - Match things and delete things.
  84/06/09. A. E. Klietz.                
  Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/room.h"
struct mo_monster *matchmonster(monstertail, test)
struct mo_monster *monstertail;
boolean (*test)();  /* Pointer to function returning boolean */
{
 register struct mo_monster *pointer;
 for (pointer = monstertail; pointer != NULL; pointer = pointer->mo_next)
  if ((*test)(pointer))
   break;
 return(pointer);
}
struct ob_object *matchobject(objecttail, test)
struct ob_object *objecttail;
boolean (*test)();  /* Pointer to function returning boolean */
{
 register struct ob_object *pointer;
 for (pointer = objecttail; pointer != NULL; pointer = pointer->ob_next)
  if ((*test)(pointer))
   break;
 return(pointer);
}
struct us_user *matchplayer(playertail, test)
struct us_user *playertail;
boolean (*test)();  /* Pointer to function returning boolean */
{
 register struct us_user *pointer;
 for (pointer = playertail; pointer != NULL; pointer = pointer->us_next)
  if ((*test)(pointer))
   break;
 return(pointer);
}
struct us_user *matchuser(usrtail, test)
struct us_user *usrtail;
boolean (*test)();  /* Pointer to function returning boolean */
{
 register struct us_user *pointer;
 for (pointer = usrtail; pointer != NULL; pointer = pointer->us_nextuser)
  if ((*test)(pointer))
   break;
 return(pointer);
}
static char *name;
static boolean namemon(mon)
struct mo_monster *mon;
{
 return(equal(name, mon->mo_name));
}
fixmoncount(monstertail, nam)
/* Restore the index numbers of monsters and one leaves
   or arrives.  Update  ->top and ->nul */
struct mo_monster *monstertail;
char *nam;
{
 register struct mo_monster *pt;
 register i, nummon;
 name = nam;
 if ((pt = matchmonster(monstertail, namemon)) != NULL) {
  nummon = 0;
  while (pt != NULL) {
   pt = matchmonster(pt->mo_next, namemon);
   ++nummon;
  }
  pt = matchmonster(monstertail, namemon);
  for (i=nummon; i >= 1; --i) {
   pt->mo_num = min(i, 9);
   pt->mo_top = (i == nummon);
   pt = matchmonster(pt->mo_next, namemon);
  }
 }
}
static struct us_user *player;
static boolean patk(plyr)
struct us_user *plyr;
{
 return(plyr->us_defplayer == player);
}
static boolean mattack(mon)
struct mo_monster *mon;
{
 return(mon->mo_defplayer == player);
}
stopotheratk(playr, rm)
/* stop attack against player that is leaving */
struct us_user *playr;
word rm;
{
 register struct us_user *otherplayer; 
 register struct mo_monster *othermonster;
 player = validusr(playr);
 player->us_defmon = NULL; /* stop player's attacks too */
 player->us_defplayer = NULL;
 otherplayer = matchplayer(room[rm].ro_rmplayertail, patk);
 while (otherplayer != NULL) {
  otherplayer->us_defplayer = NULL;
  otherplayer = matchplayer(otherplayer->us_next, patk);
 }
 othermonster = matchmonster(room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail, mattack);
 while (othermonster != NULL) {
  othermonster->mo_defplayer = NULL;
  if (othermonster->mo_mreact >= 5)
   othermonster->mo_mreact = 0;
  othermonster = matchmonster(othermonster->mo_next, mattack);
 }
}
 
static struct mo_monster *monster;
static boolean pattacking(othrplyr)
struct us_user *othrplyr;
{
 return(othrplyr->us_defmon = monster);
}
stopplyratk(mnster, rm)
/* stop attack against a monster that is leaving */
struct mo_monster *mnster;
word rm;
{
 register struct us_user *otherplayer;
 monster = mnster;
 otherplayer = matchplayer(room[rm].ro_rmplayertail, pattacking);
 while (otherplayer != NULL) {
  otherplayer->us_defmon = NULL;
  otherplayer = matchplayer(otherplayer->us_next, pattacking);
 }
}
destroy(mon)
/* destroy a monster */
struct mo_monster *mon;
{
 register struct ob_object *obj;
 while ((obj = mon->mo_objecttail) != NULL) {
  mon->mo_objecttail = obj->ob_next;
  odestroy(obj);
 }
 free(mon->mo_name);
 free(mon);
}
insertmonster(monster, rm)
/* add a monster to the tail, call fixmoncount */
struct mo_monster *monster;
word rm;
{
 monster->mo_next = room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail;
 room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail = monster;
 fixmoncount(monster, monster->mo_name);
}
deletemonster(pt, rm)
struct mo_monster *pt;
word rm;
{
 register struct mo_monster *pt2;
 if (pt == NULL)
  crash(desc(783));
  /* Sce783 - Cannot delete null monster!\n */
 if ((pt2 = room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail) == pt)
  room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail = pt2->mo_next;
 else {
  for (; pt2 != NULL; pt2 = pt2->mo_next)
   if (pt2->mo_next == pt)
    break;
  
  if (pt2 == NULL)
   crash(desc(784));
   /* Sce784 - Deleted monster not found!\n */
  else
   pt2->mo_next = pt->mo_next;
 }
 fixmoncount(room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail, pt->mo_name);
 if (room[rm].ro_rmplayertail != NULL)
  stopplyratk(pt, rm);
}
boolean deleteobject(pt, objecttail)
struct ob_object *pt, *objecttail;
{
 register struct ob_object *pt2;
 if (pt == NULL)
  crash(desc(785));
  /* Sce785 - Cannot delete null object!\n */
 if  ((pt2 = objecttail) == pt)
  return(TRUE);
 else {
  for (; pt2 != NULL; pt2 = pt2->ob_next)
   if (pt2->ob_next == pt)
    break;
  if (pt2 == NULL)
   crash(desc(786));
   /* Sce786 - Deleted object not found!\n */
  else
   pt2->ob_next = pt->ob_next;
  return(FALSE);
 }
}
cleanrm(rm)
/* Remove old monsters and objects that are not permanent */
word rm;
{
 register struct ob_object *obj;
 struct  ob_object *obj2;
 register struct mo_monster *mon;
 struct  mo_monster *mon2;
 for (obj = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail; obj != NULL; )
  if (!obj->ob_permanent) {  
   if (deleteobject(obj, room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail))
    room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail->ob_next;
   obj2 = obj->ob_next;
   odestroy(obj);
   obj = obj2;
  }
  else
   obj = obj->ob_next;
  
 for (mon = room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail; mon != NULL; )
  if (!mon->mo_permanent) {
   mon2 = mon->mo_next;
   deletemonster(mon, rm);
   destroy(mon);
   mon = mon2;
  }
  else {
   mon->mo_hits = mon->mo_maxhits;
   mon = mon->mo_next;
  }
}
deleteplayer(pt, rm)
struct us_user *pt;
word rm;
{
 register struct us_user *pt2;
 if (pt == NULL)
  crash(desc(787));
  /* Sce787 - Cannot delete null player!\n */
 if ((pt2 = room[rm].ro_rmplayertail) == pt) {
  pt2 = room[rm].ro_rmplayertail = pt2->us_next;
  if (pt2 == NULL || pt2->us_hits == 0)
   cleanrm(rm);
 }
 else {
  for (; pt2 != NULL; pt2 = pt2->us_next)
   if (pt2->us_next == pt)
    break;
  if (pt2 == NULL)
   crash(desc(788));
   /* Sce788 - Deleted player not found!\n */
  else
   pt2->us_next = pt->us_next;
 }
 
 stopotheratk(pt, rm);
 pt->us_follow = NULL;
 inactive(rm);
}
deleteuser(pt)
struct us_user *pt;
{
 register struct us_user *pt2, *followplyr;
 register kludge;
 pt = validusr(pt);
 if (pt == NULL || usertail == NULL)
  crash(desc(789));
  /* Sce789 - Cannot delete NULL user!\n */
 if ((pt2 = usertail) == pt)
  usertail = pt2->us_nextuser;
 else {
  for (; pt2 != NULL; pt2 = pt2->us_nextuser)
   if (pt2->us_nextuser == pt)
    break;
  if (pt2 == NULL)
   crash(desc(790));
   /* Sce790 - Deleted user not found!\n */
        else
   pt2->us_nextuser = pt->us_nextuser;
 }
 if (pt->us_status != SLOGIN)
  deleteplayer(pt, pt->us_rmcode);
 
/* KLUDGE DUE TO COMPILER BUG WITH BIT FIELDS */
 kludge = pt->us_rmcode;
 pt->us_rmcode = w(kludge);
 for (followplyr = usertail; followplyr != NULL; followplyr = followplyr->us_nextuser)
  if (followplyr->us_follow == pt)
   followplyr->us_follow = NULL;
}
stopusing(user, object)
struct us_user *user;
struct ob_object *object;
{
 register struct us_user  *usr;
 usr = validusr(user);
 if (object != NULL) {
  if (object == usr->us_usweap)
   usr->us_usweap = NULL;
  else
   if (object == usr->us_usshield) {
    usr->us_usshield = NULL;
    usr->us_ac += object->ob.ob_shield.ob_shplus;
   }
   else
    if (object == usr->us_usarm) {
     usr->us_usarm = NULL;
     usr->us_ac += object->ob.ob_armor.ob_armplus;
    }
 } 
}

/*   find - find things.
   84/06/12. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#define MAXNAMES 30 /* maximum number of objects in a room for parsing */
boolean lsubset(prt, fll)
/* Return TRUE if "part" is a lowercase substring of "full" */
char *prt, *fll;
{
 register char *part, *full;
 register char ch;
 part = prt;
 full = fll;
 while ((ch = *part++) == tolower(*full++) && ch != '\0')
  ;
 return(ch == '\0');
}
word wordmatch(wrd, count, namelist)
/* Return index of wrd in array.  -2 if not unique, and it
issues its own message. -1 if not found and the calling
program must issue its own message. */
char *wrd;
word count;
char *namelist[];
{
 register i,j;
 if (count < 0 || count > 255)
  crash(desc(900));
  /* Sce900 - Bad parse count!\n */
 if (wrd[0] == '\0')
  return(-1); /* empty string does not match anything */
 for (i=0; i < count; ++i)
  if (equal(wrd, namelist[i]))
   break;
 if (i < count)
  return(i); /* direct match */
 for (i=0; i < count; ++i)
  if (lsubset(wrd, namelist[i]))
   break;
 if (i >= count)
  return(-1);
 for (j=i+1; j < count; ++j)
  if (lsubset(wrd, namelist[j])) {
   char buf[BUFLEN], buf2[BUFLEN];
   word temp;
   temp = parseloc;
   reset_parse();
   parse(namelist[i], buf);
   if (equal(wrd, buf)) {
    parseloc = temp;
    return(i);
   }
   reset_parse();
   parse(namelist[j], buf2);
   if (equal(wrd, buf2)) {
    parseloc = temp;
    return(j);
   }
   parseloc = temp;
   if (!equal(buf, buf2))
    break;
  }
 if (j < count) { /* if not unique */
  mwrite(My_mask, "\"%s\" is not unique.\n", wrd);
  return(-2);
 }
 else
  return(i);
}
  
struct mo_monster *findmonster(wrd, nm, monstertail)
char *wrd;
word nm;                         
struct mo_monster *monstertail;
{
 register struct mo_monster *monster;
 char  *namelist[MAXNAMES];
 register count, index;
 extern  word parseloc;
 for (count=0, monster = monstertail; monster != NULL && count < MAXNAMES;
   ++count, monster = monster->mo_next)
  namelist[count] = monster->mo_name;
 if (count >= MAXNAMES)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(902));
  /* Sce902 - Monster table overflow.\n */
 if ((index = wordmatch(wrd, count, namelist)) < 0)
  return(NULL);
 for (monster = monstertail, count = 0; count < index;  ++count)
   monster = monster->mo_next;
 
 for (; monster != NULL; monster = monster->mo_next) 
  if ((nm == monster->mo_num || nm == 0) && 
    lsubset(wrd, monster->mo_name))
   break;
 return(monster);
}
struct ob_object *findobject(wrd, nm, objecttail)
char *wrd;
word nm;
struct ob_object *objecttail;
{
 register struct ob_object *object;
 register i;
 word  nummatch, count, index;
 char  *namelist[MAXNAMES];
 
 for (count=0, object = objecttail; object != NULL && count < MAXNAMES;
   ++count, object = object->ob_next)
  namelist[count] = object->ob_name;
 if (count >= MAXNAMES)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(903));
     /* Sce903 - Object table overflow. */
 if ((index = wordmatch(wrd, count, namelist)) < 0)
  return(NULL);
 for (object = objecttail, i = 0; i < index; ++i)
  object = object->ob_next;
 if (nm <= 1)
  return(object);
 
 nummatch = 1;
 for (i = index + 1; nummatch < nm && i < count; ++i) {
  object = object->ob_next;
  if (lsubset(wrd, namelist[i]))
   ++nummatch;
 }
 if (nummatch < nm)
  return(NULL);
 return(object);
}
struct us_user *findplayer(wrd, playertail)
char *wrd;
struct us_user *playertail;
{
 register struct us_user *player;
 register count, i, index;
 char  *namelist[MAXNAMES];
 
 for (count=0, player = playertail; player != NULL && count < MAXNAMES;
   ++count, player = player->us_next)
  namelist[count] = player->us_name;
 if (count >= MAXNAMES)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(904));
     /* Sce904 - player table overflow. */
 if ((index = wordmatch(wrd, count, namelist)) < 0)
  return(NULL);
 for (player = playertail, i = 0; i < index; ++i)
  player = player->us_next;
 if (player != NULL && player->us_nonexistant && !user->us_ssj)
  return(NULL);
 return(player);
}
struct us_user *finduser(wrd, usrtail)
char *wrd;
struct us_user *usrtail;
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register count, i, index;
 char  *namelist[MAXNAMES];
 
 for (count=0, usr = usrtail; usr != NULL && count < MAXNAMES;
   ++count, usr = usr->us_nextuser)
  namelist[count] = usr->us_name;
 if (count >= MAXNAMES)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(905));
     /* Sce905 - user table overflow. */
 if ((index = wordmatch(wrd, count, namelist)) < 0)
  return(NULL);
 for (usr = usrtail, i = 0; i < index; ++i)
  usr = usr->us_nextuser;
 if (usr != NULL && usr->us_nonexistant && !user->us_ssj)
  return(NULL);
 return(usr);
}
                              
char *printobj(obj, singular)
/* Display the name of an object.  If singular = TRUE then
display "the", else the article (a, an) */
struct ob_object *obj;
boolean singular;
{
 register loc;
 char  buf2[BUFLEN], buf3[BUFLEN];
 static  char buf[160];
 register char  *txt;
 register struct ob_object *object;
 word  temparse;
 extern  word parseloc;
 object = obj;
 loc = 0;

  strncpy(&buf[loc], "the ", 160 - loc);
  loc += 4;
 }
 if (!singular) 
  if (object->ob_obclass != DOOR) {
   switch (object->ob_article) {
    case A: txt = "a ";
    break;
    case AN: txt = "an ";
    break;
 
    case THE: txt = "the ";
    break;
    case SOME: txt = "some ";
    break;
    default:
    case NONE: txt = "";
    break;
   }
   strncpy(&buf[loc], txt, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(txt);

    case A:
    case AN:
     if (object->ob.ob_door.ob_dclosed)
      txt = "a ";
     else
      txt = "an ";
    break;
    case THE: txt = "the ";
    break;
    case SOME: txt = "some ";
    break;
    default:
    case NONE: txt = "";
    break;

   strncpy(&buf[loc], txt, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(txt);
   if (object->ob.ob_door.ob_dclosed)
    if (object->ob.ob_door.ob_dlocked > 0)
     txt = "locked ";
    else
     txt = "closed ";
   else
    txt = "open ";
   strncpy(&buf[loc], txt, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(txt);

 if (object->ob_obclass == COINS) {
  tsprintf(buf2, "%u ", object->ob_price / object->ob.ob_coins.ob_multiplier);
  strncpy(&buf[loc], buf2, 160 - loc);
  loc += strlen(buf2);
 }
 if (object->ob_magic) {
  strncpy(&buf[loc], "magic ", 160 - loc);
  loc += 6;
 }
 temparse = parseloc;  
 reset_parse();
 upper_parse(object->ob_name, buf2);
 if (parseloc > 0) {
  txt = object->ob_name + parseloc;
  tsprintf(buf3, txt, buf2);  
  txt = buf3;
 }
 else
  txt = buf2;
 parseloc = temparse;
 strncpy(&buf[loc], txt, 160 - loc);
 loc += strlen(txt);

   strncpy(&buf[loc], " (inv)", 160 - loc);
   loc += 6;

   tsprintf(buf2, " (+%d)", abs(object->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus));
   strncpy(&buf[loc], buf2, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(buf2);
  }

   tsprintf(buf2, " (%d)", abs(object->ob.ob_shield.ob_shplus));
   strncpy(&buf[loc], buf2, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(buf2);

   tsprintf(buf2, " (value: %u)", object->ob_price);
   strncpy(&buf[loc], buf2, 160 - loc);
   loc += strlen(buf2);
  }
 }
 return(buf);

/*  validusr - validate user record

*/

struct us_user *validusr(usr)
struct us_user *usr;
{
 if (usr == NULL)
  crash(desc(1010));
  /* Sce1010 - NULL user pointer!\n */
 if (usr->us_checkword != CHECKWORD)
  crash(desc(1011));
  /* Sce1011 - Bad checkword on user pointer!\n */
 return(usr);
}

char *pnth(n)
word n;
{
 char *suffix;
 if (n >= 0 && n <= 20)
  return(ordinal[n]);
 switch (n % 10) {
case 1: suffix = "st"; break;
case 2: suffix = "nd"; break;
case 3: suffix = "rd"; break;
default: suffix = "th"; break;
 }
 tsprintf(nth, "%.7d%.2s", n, suffix);
 return(nth);
}
char *pn(n)

{
 
 if (n >= 0 && n <= 20)
  return(numeral[n]);
 tsprintf(nm, "%.9d", n);
 return(nm);
}

/*   display - display things.
   84/06/12. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
extern char *cname[];
static char *tf[] = {
 "False",
 "True" };
objdisplay(obj)
struct ob_object *obj;
{
 register struct ob_object *object, *tobj;
 char  *name;
 object = obj;
 name = printobj(object, FALSE);
 name[0] = toupper(name[0]);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%s, %s%d lbs, %u shillings.\n", name,
  (object->ob_carry ? "carryable, " : ""),
  object->ob_weight,
  object->ob_price);
 if (object->ob_desccode != 0 
   && (user->us_ssj || object->ob_obclass != SCROLL)) 
  mwrite(My_mask, "Descrip (%d): %s\n", object->ob_desccode,
   printdesc(object->ob_desccode, 0, FALSE));
 
 mwrite(My_mask, "Magic=%s, Permanent=%s, Invisible=%s\n",
  tf[object->ob_magic], tf[object->ob_permanent],
  tf[object->ob_invisible]);
 switch (object->ob_obclass) {
 
  case PORTAL:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Portal to room %d.\n", 
    object->ob.ob_portal.ob_towhere);
   break;
  case WEAP:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Offensive weapon, %d-%d hp, %d strikes left.\n",
    object->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp, object->ob.ob_weap.ob_maxhp,
    object->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft);
   switch (object->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaptype) {
    case SHARP: mwrite(My_mask, "Sharp ");
    break;
    case THRUST: mwrite(My_mask, "Thrust ");
    break;
    case BLUNT: mwrite(My_mask, "Blunt ");
    break;
    case LONG: mwrite(My_mask, "Pole ");
    break;
   }
   mwrite(My_mask, "class weapon,  +%d to hit.\n", 
    object->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus);
   break;
 
  case SHIELD:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Shield, +%d protection,  %d hits left.\n",
    object->ob.ob_shield.ob_shplus, object->ob.ob_shield.ob_shhitsleft);
   break;
  case ARMOR:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Armor, +%d protection,  %d hits left.\n",
    object->ob.ob_armor.ob_armplus, object->ob.ob_armor.ob_armhitsleft);
   break;
  case COINS:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Money, value multiplier: X%d.\n", 
    object->ob.ob_coins.ob_multiplier);
   break;
  case SCROLL:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Scroll, spell = %s\n",
    spellist[object->ob.ob_scroll.ob_spell]);
   break;
  case CHEST:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Container, objects=%d, trap=%d\n",
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_numinside,
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_trap);
   mwrite(My_mask, "Lock type = %d.\n", object->ob.ob_chest.ob_locked);
   if ((tobj = object->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail) != NULL) { 
    mwrite(My_mask, "Items inside:\n");
    while (tobj != NULL) {
     mwrite(My_mask, "    %s\n", printobj(tobj, FALSE));
     tobj = tobj->ob_next;
    }
   }
   break;
  case DOOR:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Door, portal to room %d, door trap = %d.\n",
    object->ob.ob_door.ob_dtowhere, object->ob.ob_door.ob_dtrap);
   mwrite(My_mask, "Dlock type=%d.\n", object->ob.ob_door.ob_dlocked);
   break;
  case MAGDEVICE:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Magical device, spell = %s.\n",
    spellist[object->ob.ob_magdevice.ob_mspell]);
   mwrite(My_mask, "Number of charges = %d.\n",
    object->ob.ob_magdevice.ob_numcharges);
  break;
  case TELEPORT:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Teleport device, from room %d to room %d.\n",
    object->ob.ob_teleport.ob_tactiverm,
    object->ob.ob_teleport.ob_towhere);
   break;
  case KEYS:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Keys, unlock type=%d.\n",object->ob.ob_keys.ob_unlock);
   break;
  case CARD:
   mwrite(My_mask, "Cards, teleport to player.\n");
   break;
  default:
   mwrite(My_mask, "\n");
   break;
 }
}
longword expr(lev)
word lev;
{
 register num;
 num = lev;
 if (num <= 1)
  return(0L);
 if (num <= 7)
  return ((longword) (1 << (num+7)));
 if (num == 8)
  return(32768L);
 if (num == 9)
  return(65536L);
 if (num == 10)
  return(131072L);
 return(262144L * (longword) (num - 10));
}
playerdisplay(plyer)
struct us_user *plyer;
{
 register count;
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register struct ob_object *obj;
 char  *name;
 usr = validusr(plyer);
 usr->us_name[0] = toupper(usr->us_name[0]);
 mwrite(My_mask,"%s, the %s level %s", 
  usr->us_name, pnth(usr->us_lvl), cname[usr->us_class]);
 usr->us_name[0] = tolower(usr->us_name[0]);
 if (usr->us_playtester)
  mwrite(My_mask, " (Playtester)");
 if (usr->us_ssj && !usr->us_assoc && !usr->us_master)
  mwrite(My_mask, " (DM)");
 if (usr->us_ssj && usr->us_assoc)
  mwrite(My_mask, " (Assoc DM)");
 if (usr->us_ssj && usr->us_master)
  mwrite(My_mask, " (Master DM)");
 if (user->us_ssj)
  mwrite(My_mask, " in room %d/%d", usr->us_rmcode,
   w(usr->us_rmcode));
 mwrite(My_mask, "\n");
 if (usr->us_invisible)
  if (usr->us_nonexistant)
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(801));
   /* You are nonexistant. */
  else
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(800));
   /* You are invisible */
 if (usr->us_tguild)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(803));
  /* You are a member of the thieves guild. */
 if (usr->us_aguild)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(804));
  /* You are a member of the assassins guild. */
 if (usr->us_evil)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(805));
  /* You have fallen into the forces of evil. */
 
 mwrite(My_mask, "with %d/%d Vitality pts, ", usr->us_hits,
  usr->us_maxhits);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%d/%d Fatigue pts, and %d/%d Magic pts.\n",
  usr->us_fatigue, usr->us_maxfatigue, usr->us_magic, usr->us_maxmagic);
 if (usr->us_poisoned)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(806));
  /* You are dying from poison! */
 mwrite(My_mask, desc(807), pn(usr->us_ac));
  /* You have a defensive armor class of %s.\n */
 mwrite(My_mask, "Str=%d, Int=%d, Dex=%d, Pty=%d,",
  usr->us_str, usr->us_int, usr->us_dex, usr->us_pty);
 mwrite(My_mask, " Con=%d.\n", usr->us_con);
 mwrite(My_mask, "Weapon skill: Sharp-%d%%, Thrust-%d%%, Blunt-%d%%, Pole-%d%%\n",
  usr->us_ssharp * 10, usr->us_sthrust * 10, usr->us_sblunt * 10,
  usr->us_slong * 10);
{
 longword pts;
 if ((pts = expr(usr->us_lvl + 1) - expr(usr->us_lvl) - usr->us_experience)
  < 0L)
  pts = 0L;
 mwritef(My_mask, desc(808), pts);
 /* You need %ld more exp points to reach the next level. */
}
 mwritef(My_mask, desc(809), usr->us_money);
 /* You have %ld shillings in cash. */
 mwritef(My_mask, desc(810), usr->us_weight);
 /* You are carrying %d lbs of items. */
 count = 0;
 obj = usr->us_objecttail;
 while (obj != NULL) {
  ++count;
  name = printobj(obj, FALSE);
  name[0] = toupper(name[0]);
  mwrite(My_mask,"   %d) %s\n", count, name);
  obj = obj->ob_next;
 }
 if (count > MAXOBJS)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(811), pn(MAXOBJS));
  /* Only the first %s items will be saved when you log out.\n */
}
mondisplay(monster)
struct mo_monster *monster;
{
 register struct mo_monster *mon;
 register struct ob_object *object;
 char *str;
 mon = monster;
 str = pm(mon);
 str[0] = toupper(str[0]);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%s is a %s level monster with %d/%d HP\n", 
  str, pnth(mon->mo_lvl), mon->mo_hits, mon->mo_maxhits);
 mwrite(My_mask, "Defend=%s, Block=%s, Follow=%s,\n",
  tf[mon->mo_defend], tf[mon->mo_block], tf[mon->mo_follow]);
 mwrite(My_mask, "Guard=%s, Atklastaggr=%s, Permanent=%s.\n",
  tf[mon->mo_guard], tf[mon->mo_atklastaggr], tf[mon->mo_permanent]);
 mwrite(My_mask, "%d Experience pts.\n", mon->mo_experience);
 if (mon->mo_moralreact)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(820));
  /* Monster will hit players with piety less than eight.\n */
 if (mon->mo_invisible)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(821));
  /* Monster is invisible.\n */
 if (mon->mo_flee)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(822));
  /* Monster may flee if attacked. */
 if (mon->mo_assistance)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(823));
  /* Monster will call for help if attacked. */
 if (mon->mo_magic)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(824));
  /* Affected only by magical weapons.\n */
 if (mon->mo_poison)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(825));
  /* Monster is poisonous. */
 if (mon->mo_antimagic)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(826));
  /* Monster is immune to spells.\n */
 if (mon->mo_undead)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(827));
  /* Monster is undead.\n */
 if (mon->mo_slowreact)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(828));
  /* Monster will react slowly.\n */
 if (mon->mo_fastreact)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(829));
  /* Monster will react quickly.\n */
 if (mon->mo_regenerate)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(830));
  /* Monster can regenerate hp.\n */
 if (mon->mo_drain)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(831));
  /* Monster can drain levels from players!\n */
 if (mon->mo_monspells)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(832));
  /* Monster can cast spells.\n */
 if (mon->mo_nokill)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(839));
  /* Monster should not be killed.\n */
 if (mon->mo_kidnap)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1840));
  /* Monster can kidnap players.\n */
 if (mon->mo_mparley >0)
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(833), pn(mon->mo_mparley));

/*   display2 - display more things.
   84/06/12. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"

extern char *dirlist[];
punctuate(num, total)
{
 if (num != total)
  if (total - num >= 2)
   mwrite(My_mask, ", ");
  else
   mwrite(My_mask, " and ");
 if (num % 3 == 0 && num < total)
  mwrite(My_mask, "\n");
}
inventory()
{
 register i, count;
 register struct ob_object *object;
 char *name;
 mwrite(My_mask, desc(840));
 /* You are carrying the following objects:\n */
 count = 0;
 object = user->us_objecttail;
 while (object != NULL) {
  ++count;
  object = object->ob_next;
 }
 if (count > 0) {
  object = user->us_objecttail;
  i = 0;
  while (i < count && object != NULL) {
   ++i;  
   if (i == 1) {
    name = printobj(object, FALSE);
    name[0] = toupper(name[0]);
    mwrite(My_mask, "%s", name);
   }
   else
    mwrite(My_mask, "%s", printobj(object, FALSE));
   punctuate(i, count);
   object = object->ob_next;
  }
  mwrite(My_mask, ".\n\n");
 }
 else
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(841));
  /* Nothing at all.\n */
}

hitsharmor(player, damage)
/* be sure to call with &damage pointer. */
struct us_user *player;
word *damage;  /* pointer */
{
 register struct us_user  *plyr;
 register struct ob_object *temp;
 plyr = validusr(player);
 if (plyr->us_usarm != NULL) {
  word kludge;
  temp = plyr->us_usarm;
  *damage = max(0, (int) (*damage - temp->ob.ob_armor.ob_armplus));
  kludge = temp->ob.ob_armor.ob_armhitsleft; 
  temp->ob.ob_armor.ob_armhitsleft = max(0, kludge - 1);
  if (kludge - 1 <= 0) {
   mwrite(1 << plyr->us_trm, desc(1297));
   /* Your armor falls apart!\n */
   if (deleteobject(temp, plyr->us_objecttail))
    plyr->us_objecttail = plyr->us_objecttail->ob_next;
   kludge = plyr->us_weight;
   plyr->us_weight = max(0, (int) (kludge - temp->ob_weight));
   stopusing(plyr, temp);
   odestroy(temp);
  }
 }
 if (plyr->us_usshield != NULL) {
  word kludge;
  temp = plyr->us_usshield;
  kludge = temp->ob.ob_shield.ob_shhitsleft;
  temp->ob.ob_shield.ob_shhitsleft = max(0, kludge - 1);
  if (kludge - 1 <= 0) {
   mwrite(1 << plyr->us_trm, desc(1298));
   /* Your shield breaks in half!\n */
   if (deleteobject(temp, plyr->us_objecttail))
    plyr->us_objecttail = plyr->us_objecttail->ob_next;
   kludge = plyr->us_weight;
   plyr->us_weight = max(0, (int) (kludge - temp->ob_weight));
   stopusing(plyr, temp);
   odestroy(temp);
  }

/*  protectnpc
  84/08/01.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
protectnpc(player, monster)
struct us_user *player;
struct mo_monster *monster;
{
 register struct us_user *plyr;
 register struct ob_object *temp;
 register i, mask;
 plyr = validusr(player);
 mask = 1 << plyr->us_trm;
 mwrite(mask, desc(2100), monster->mo_name);
 /* As you step forward to attack, the %s yells */
 mwrite(mask, desc(2101));
 /* "Help! Help! Guards! Guards!"... in seconds several\n */
 mwrite(mask, desc(2102));
 /* burly guards grab you, and take away your weapon!\n */
 if (plyr->us_usweap != NULL) {
  temp = plyr->us_usweap;
  plyr->us_usweap = NULL;
  if (deleteobject(temp, plyr->us_objecttail))
   plyr->us_objecttail = plyr->us_objecttail->ob_next;
  i = plyr->us_weight;
  plyr->us_weight = max(0, (int) (i - temp->ob_weight));
  odestroy(temp);
 }
 if (plyr->us_lvl <= 2) {
  mwrite(mask, desc(2103));
  /* But since you are a young, inexperienced adventurer, they\ngive you a stern warning a decide not to throw you in jail.\n */
  mwrite(Local_mask, desc(2104), genuser[plyr->us_trm].gn_name,
   monster->mo_name, plyr->us_sex ? "her" : "him");
  /* %s assaults the %s, but the town guards stop %s\n */
 }
 else {
  mwrite(mask, desc(2105));
  /* Then they throw you in jail!\n */
  mwrite(Allbutme_mask, desc(2106), genuser[plyr->us_trm].gn_name,
   monster->mo_name);
  /* ### %s was thrown in jail for assaulting the %s.\n */
  deleteplayer(plyr, plyr->us_rmcode);  
  placeplayer(plyr, 5); /* jail */
  mwrite(mask, "\n");
  i = My_mask;
  My_mask = mask;
  roomdisplay(plyr->us_rmcode, FALSE);
  My_mask = i;
 }
 plyr->us_defmon = NULL;
}

  return(NULL);
 }
 else
  return(usr->us_usweap);

#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"

#include "^^def/room.h"
extern word objlistlen;
extern word (*objindex)[6];
maketreasure(monster, rm)
struct mo_monster *monster;
word rm;
{
 boolean nothing;
 register struct ob_object *obj, *lastobj;
 register i;
 word numobjs, obnum;
 float factor;
 nothing = TRUE;
 mwrite(My_mask, "On %s you find:\n", pm(monster));
 if (monster->mo_objecttail != NULL) {
  nothing = FALSE;
  lastobj = monster->mo_objecttail;
  while (lastobj->ob_next != NULL)
   lastobj = lastobj->ob_next;
  lastobj->ob_next = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail;
  lastobj = lastobj->ob_next;
  room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail = monster->mo_objecttail;
  monster->mo_objecttail = NULL;
 }
 else
  lastobj = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail;
 if (monster->mo_whichobj >= objlistlen) {
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(2149), monster->mo_whichobj);
  /* Sce2149 - Object list index %d out of bounds!\n */
  monster->mo_whichobj = 0;
 }
 if (monster->mo_whichobj > 0) {
  if (ran(4) == 1)
   numobjs = ran(4);
  else
   numobjs = 1;
 
  for (i=0; i < numobjs; ++i) {
   obnum = objindex[monster->mo_whichobj][ran(6)-1];
   if (obnum > 0) {
    unsigned kludge;
    nothing = FALSE;
    obj = get_olist(obnum);
    factor = (ran(40) + 80) / 100.0;
    kludge = obj->ob_price;
    obj->ob_price = (unsigned) (kludge * factor);
    obj->ob_next = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail;
    room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail = obj;
   }
  }
 }
 if (nothing)
  mwrite(My_mask, "   Nothing.\n");
 else {
  obj = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail;
  do {
   char *string;
  
   string = printobj(obj, FALSE);
   string[0] = toupper(string[0]);
   mwrite(My_mask, "   %s\n", string);
   obj = obj->ob_next;
  } while (obj != lastobj);
 }
}

    obj->ob_price = (unsigned) (kludge * factor);

/*  hitplayer.c
  84/08/02. A. E. Klietz
  Copyright (c) 1984 by Alan E. Klietz.    All Rights Reserved.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
extern char *cname[];
hitplayer(damage)
word damage;
{
 register struct us_user *player;
 register struct mo_monster *revenent;
 boolean  merc, cold;
 register i;
 unsigned plyr_mask;
 player = user->us_defplayer;
 plyr_mask = 1 << player->us_trm;
 hitsharmor(player, &damage);
 mwrite(My_mask, "You hit %s for ", genuser[player->us_trm].gn_name);
 printdmg(player, damage, user->us_brief, My_mask);
 mwrite(plyr_mask, "%s hits you for ", genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name);
 printdmg(player, damage, player->us_brief, plyr_mask);
 if (player->us_hits + player->us_fatigue <= damage)
  if (secdiff(&player->us_hitattime, &realtime) <= 30) {
   mwrite(My_mask, "You killed %s!\n", pro[player->us_sex]);
   mwrite(plyr_mask, "You're dead, %s!\n", genuser[player->us_trm].gn_name);
   merc = (((int) (user->us_lvl - player->us_lvl)) > 3);
   cold = (player->us_defplayer == NULL);
   if (player->us_lvl > 5)
    daymsg("Kil", player->us_name, player->us_lvl);
   mwrite(All_mask, "### %s just killed %s%s%s!\n",
    genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name, genuser[player->us_trm].gn_name,
    merc ? " mercilessly" : "",
    cold ? " in cold blood" : "");
{
   word kludge;
   kludge = user->us_pty;
   if (merc) 
    kludge = max(-10, kludge - 1);
   if (cold)
    kludge = max(-10, kludge - 1);
   if (user->us_aguild)
    kludge = max(0, kludge - 1);
   user->us_pty = kludge;
  
}
   if (user->us_class == PALADIN && (merc || cold) &&
     player->us_class != THIEF)
    user->us_class = FIGHTER;
   if (player->us_con >= 18) { /* If strong enough for revenent */
    revenent = get_mlist(1);
    insertmonster(revenent, user->us_rmcode);
    revenent->mo_hits = player->us_maxhits;
    revenent->mo_maxhits = player->us_maxhits;
    revenent->mo_lvl = player->us_lvl;
    revenent->mo_defplayer = user;
    mwrite(My_mask, desc(2438));
    /* From the dead body you see a gaseous shape form...\n */
   }
   player->us_dead = TRUE;
   player->us_fatigue = 0;
   player->us_hits = 0;  /* Kill player */
   if ((merc || cold) && (-(((int) user->us_pty) * 10) > ran(100))) {
    mwrite(My_mask, desc(2349));
    /* Enough is enough!  The people rise up against you in an angry mob!\nYou have been lynched!\n */
    daymsg("Lyn", "", 0);
    mwrite(All_mask, desc(2350), genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name,
     cname[user->us_class]);
    /* ### %s, the %s, was just lynched by an angry mob.\n */
    user->us_dead = TRUE; user->us_hits = 0;
    user->us_fatigue = 0;
   }
  }
  else {
   player->us_fatigue = 0;
   player->us_hits = 1;
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(2351));
   /* You hear a voice cry out, "Run, Fool, Run!"\n */
  }
 else
  if (damage > player->us_fatigue) {
   word kludge;
   kludge = player->us_hits;
   player->us_hits = max(0, kludge - damage + (int) player->us_fatigue);
   kludge = player->us_fatigue;
   player->us_fatigue = max(0, kludge - damage);
  }
}
struct ob_object *get_fist()
{
 register struct ob_object *fist;
 char  *string;
 fist = alloc(sizeof(struct ob_object));
 string = "fist";
 fist->ob_name = alloc(strlen(string)+1);
 strncpy(fist->ob_name, string, BUFLEN);
 fist->ob_obclass = WEAP;
 fist->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp = 1;
 fist->ob.ob_weap.ob_maxhp = 3;
 fist->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft = 100;
 fist->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaptype = BLUNT;
 fist->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus = -2;
 return(fist);
}
hitatplayer(weapon, plus)
struct ob_object *weapon;
word plus;
{
 word damage;
 boolean withfist;
 register struct us_user *usr;
 usr = validusr(user);
 withfist = FALSE;
 if (weapon == NULL) {
  withfist = TRUE;
  weapon = get_fist();
 }
 
 if ((ran(20) != 20) && (ran(20) < 24 + ((int) usr->us_defplayer->us_lvl) 
  - ((int) usr->us_lvl) - ((int) weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus)
  - ((int) usr->us_str) / 6
  - ((int) usr->us_defplayer->us_ac)
  + ((int) usr->us_defplayer->us_dex) / 6 - plus)
  || ((int) usr->us_defplayer->us_hits) == 0) {
  if (withfist)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Woosh!  Your fist misses!\n");
  else
   mwrite(My_mask, "You missed!\n");
  mwrite(1 << usr->us_defplayer->us_trm, "%s misses you!\n",
   genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_name);
 }
 else {
  word kludge;
  kludge = weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft;
  weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft = kludge - 1;
  damage = max(0, ((int) weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp) + 
   ran(((int) weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_maxhp)
   - ((int) weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp) + 1)
   + ((int) weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus) + plus);
  if (withfist)
   mwrite(My_mask, "Pow!\n");
  hitplayer(damage);
 }
 if (usr->us_defplayer != NULL)
  time(&(usr->us_defplayer->us_hitattime));
 if (withfist)
  odestroy(weapon);
}

/*  hitmonster.c
  84/08/02. A. E. Klietz
  Copyright (c) 1984 by Alan E. Klietz.    All Rights Reserved.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
hitmonster(damage)
word damage;
{
 register struct mo_monster *monster;
 register struct us_user  *usr;
 word  rm;
 register i;
 usr = validusr(user);
 rm = usr->us_rmcode;
 monster = usr->us_defmon;
 if (((int) monster->mo_hits) - damage <= 0) {
  mwrite(My_mask, "You killed the %s!\n", monster->mo_name);
  if (Local_mask != 0)
   mwrite(Local_mask, "%s just killed %s!\n", genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_name,
    pm(monster));
  shareexperience(monster, rm);
  maketreasure(monster, rm);
  deletemonster(monster, rm);
  destroy(monster);
  usr->us_defmon = NULL;
 }
 else {
  if ((i = monster->mo_mparley) == 1 || i == 3 || i == 4)
   if (!monster->mo_permanent)
    monster->mo_mparley = 2;
  i = monster->mo_hits;
  monster->mo_hits = max(0, i - damage);
  if (usr->us_brief)
   mwrite(My_mask, "You hit it for %d hit points!\n", damage);
  else
   mwrite(My_mask, "You hit the %s for %d hit points!\n", monster->mo_name,
    damage);
  if (monster->mo_flee || usr->us_lvl >= 5 + monster->mo_lvl)
   if (((float) monster->mo_hits) / monster->mo_maxhits < 0.01 * ran(40)) {
    if (Local_mask != 0) {
     char *string;
     string = pm(monster);
     string[0] = toupper(string[0]);
     mwrite(Local_mask, "%s flees from %s's attack!\n", string,
      genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_name);
    }
    mwrite(My_mask, "The %s flees from your attack!\n",
     monster->mo_name);
    monster->mo_experience = monster->mo_experience / 2;
    if (usr->us_lvl < 5 + monster->mo_lvl)
     shareexperience(monster, rm);
    deletemonster(monster, rm);
    destroy(monster);
    usr->us_defmon = NULL;
   }
 }
}
hitatmonster(weapon, plus)
struct ob_object *weapon;
word plus;
{
 register struct ob_object *weap;
 register struct mo_monster *defmon;
 word  damage;
 boolean  withfist;
 defmon = user->us_defmon;
 if (defmon->mo_nokill) {
  user->us_defmon = NULL;
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(838), defmon->mo_name);
  /* Aw, don't attack the poor %s.\n */
  return;
 }
 weap = weapon;
 withfist = FALSE;
 if (weapon == NULL) {
  withfist = TRUE;
  weapon = get_fist();
  weap = weapon;
 }
 if (defmon->mo_magic && !weap->ob_magic) 
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(2455));
  /* Your weapon strikes with no effect!\n */
 else {
  if (user->us_class == PALADIN)
   if (defmon->mo_slowreact || defmon->mo_fastreact)
    ++plus;
  if (user->us_class == FIGHTER)
   ++plus;
  if (user->us_lvl == 1)
   plus += 2;
  if (defmon->mo_invisible)
   plus -= 4;
  if (defmon->mo_defend && defmon->mo_defplayer == NULL
   || defmon->mo_atklastaggr && defmon->mo_defplayer != user) {
   defmon->mo_defplayer = user;
   if (defmon->mo_mreact >= 5)
    defmon->mo_mreact = 0;
  }
  if (!defmon->mo_permanent) {
   register i;
   if ((i = defmon->mo_mparley) == 1 || i == 3 || i == 4)
    defmon->mo_mparley = 2;
  }
  if (ran(20) != 20 && ran(20) < 12 + ((int) defmon->mo_lvl)
   - ((int) user->us_lvl)
   - ((int) weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus)
   - ((int) user->us_str) / 6 - plus)
   if (withfist)
    mwrite(My_mask, "Your fist misses!\n");
   else
    mwrite(My_mask, "You missed!\n");
  else {
   register i;
   i = weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft;
   weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_strikesleft = i - 1;
   damage = max(0, ((int) weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp) +
    ran(((int) weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_maxhp)
    - ((int) weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_minhp) + 1)
    + ((int) weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaplus) + plus);
   if (withfist)
    mwrite(My_mask, "Punch!\n");
   switch (ran(100 - ((int) user->us_lvl))) {
case 1:
case 2:
case 3:
case 4:
    mwrite(My_mask, "Double damage!\n");
    damage *= 2;
    break;
case 90:
    mwrite(My_mask, "You fumbled!\n");
    damage = 0;  user->us_usweap = NULL;
    break;
   }
   if (damage >= defmon->mo_hits)
    if ((((int) (defmon->mo_lvl - user->us_lvl))) * 5 > ran(25)  
     && !user->us_skillnew) {
     register i;
     user->us_skillnew = TRUE;
     switch (weap->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaptype) {
case SHARP: if (ran(i = user->us_ssharp) == 1) 
    user->us_ssharp = min(7, i + 1);
   break;
case THRUST: if (ran(i = user->us_sthrust) == 1)
    user->us_sthrust = min(7, i + 1);
   break;
case BLUNT: if (ran(i = user->us_sblunt) == 1)
    user->us_sblunt = min(7, i + 1);
   break;
case LONG: if (ran(i = user->us_slong) == 1) 
    user->us_slong = min(7, i + 1);
   break;
     }
    }
   hitmonster(damage);
        }
 }

/*  attack.c
  84/08/02. A. E. Klietz
  Copyright (c) 1984 by Alan E. Klietz.    All Rights Reserved.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#include "^^def/room.h"
static enum towhattype {TONONE, TOMONSTER, TOPLAYER} towhat;
static word rm;
int attackwhat(worde)
char *worde;
{
 register struct us_user *olddefplayer;
 register struct mo_monster *olddefmon;
 register i;
 char  dummy[BUFLEN];
 olddefplayer = user->us_defplayer;
 user->us_defplayer = findplayer(worde, room[rm].ro_rmplayertail);
 if (user->us_defplayer == user)
  user->us_defplayer = NULL;
 if (user->us_defplayer == NULL) {
  olddefmon = user->us_defmon;
  if (num == 0)
   getword(dummy, &num);
  user->us_defmon = findmonster(worde, num, room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail);
  if (user->us_defmon == NULL) {
       if (findobject(worde, num, room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail) != NULL)
    mwrite(My_mask, desc(2345));
    /* Use the command 'smash' instead.\n */
   else
    mwrite(My_mask, "%s isn't here.\n", worde);
   return(TONONE);
  }
  else {
   if (olddefmon != user->us_defmon)
    if (Local_mask != 0)
     mwrite(Local_mask, "%s attacks %s!\n",
      genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name, pm(user->us_defmon));
   return(TOMONSTER);
  }
 }
 else {
  if (olddefplayer != user->us_defplayer) {
   i = 1 << user->us_defplayer->us_trm;
   mwrite(i, "%s is attacking you!\n",
    genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name);
   if ((Local_mask & ~i) != 0)
    mwrite(Local_mask & ~i, "%s attacks %s!\n",
     genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name, 
     genuser[user->us_defplayer->us_trm].gn_name);
  }
  return(TOPLAYER);
 }
}
 
attack(cmdcode, rmnum)
word cmdcode, rmnum;
{
 word plus;
 register struct ob_object *weapon;
 rm = rmnum;
    towhat = TONONE;
 weapon = selectweap();
 if (readycheck(&user->us_lastatk) && (weapon != NULL
  || user->us_usweap == NULL)) {
  if (room[rm].ro_safe)
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(2346));
   /* This is a safe haven.\n */
  else 
   if (wrd[0] == '\0') 
    if (user->us_defmon != NULL || user->us_defplayer != NULL) 
     if (user->us_defmon != NULL)
      towhat = TOMONSTER;
     else
      towhat = TOPLAYER;
    else
     mwrite(My_mask, desc(2347));
     /* Specify target, please!\n */
   else
    towhat = attackwhat(wrd);
  if (towhat != TONONE)
   if (cmdcode == 42 || user->us_fatigue > 0) {
    plus = 0;
    switch (cmdcode) {
case 11:   plus = user->us_fatigue;
     user->us_fatigue = max(0, plus - ran(2) + 1);
     plus = 0;
     break;
case 42: /* parry */ plus = -((int) user->us_lvl / 2 + 1);
     if (towhat == TOMONSTER)
      user->us_defmon->mo_mreact = 5; /* half damage */
     break;
case 43: /* thrust*/ plus = user->us_fatigue;
     user->us_fatigue = max(0, (int) (plus - user->us_lvl*2));
     if (towhat == TOMONSTER)
      if (ran(3) == 3) {
       user->us_defmon->mo_mreact = 6; /* double */
       mwrite(My_mask, "You are vulnerable!\n");
      }
     plus = min(user->us_lvl, 4);
     break;
case 85: /* feint */ mwrite(My_mask, "Ok\n"); 
      if (user->us_dex > ran(30))
      if (towhat == TOMONSTER)
       user->us_defmon->mo_mreact = 3;
      else {
       addtime(&realtime, 10, &(user->us_defplayer->us_lastatk));
       mwrite(1 << user->us_defplayer->us_trm, "%s feints at you!\n",
        genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name);
       mwrite(My_mask, "%s falls back!\n",
        genuser[user->us_defplayer->us_trm].gn_name);
      }
       else
      mwrite(My_mask, "Didn't work!\n");
       towhat = TONONE;
       break;
case 86: /* circle */ mwrite(My_mask, "Ok\n");
     if (user->us_dex > ran(20))
      if (towhat == TOMONSTER)
       user->us_defmon->mo_mreact = 2;
      else {
       addtime(&realtime, 8, &(user->us_defplayer->us_lastatk));
       mwrite(1 << user->us_defplayer->us_trm, "%s circles you!\n",
        genuser[user->us_trm].gn_name);
       mwrite(My_mask, "You circle %s!\n",
        pro[user->us_defplayer->us_sex]);
      }
     else
      mwrite(My_mask, "Didn't work!\n");
     towhat = TONONE;
     break;
case 72: /* backstab */ if (user->us_hidden)
      plus = 5;
     else {
      plus = -5;
      mwrite(My_mask, "You are discovered!\n");
      if (towhat == TOMONSTER) /* double dmg */
       user->us_defmon->mo_mreact = 6;
     }
     break;
    }     
    if (weapon != NULL) 
     switch (weapon->ob.ob_weap.ob_weaptype) {
case SHARP:  plus += user->us_ssharp; break;
case THRUST: plus += user->us_sthrust; break;
case BLUNT:  plus += user->us_sblunt; break;
case LONG:   plus += user->us_slong; break;
     }
    if (towhat == TOPLAYER)
     addtime(&realtime, 8, &user->us_lastatk);
    else
     addtime(&realtime, 5, &user->us_lastatk);
    user->us_hidden = FALSE;
    if (towhat == TOMONSTER)
     if (user->us_defmon->mo_assistance)
      protectnpc(user, user->us_defmon);
     else
      hitatmonster(weapon, plus);
    else
     if (towhat == TOPLAYER)
      hitatplayer(weapon, plus);
   }
   else 
    mwrite(My_mask, "You're too exhausted to strike!\n");
 }
}
;
     break;

/*  Logoff - log off the user.
  84/06/08. A. E. Klietz.
  Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
extern word nusers;
logoff(usrptr)
struct us_user  *usrptr;
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register kludge;
 usr = validusr(usrptr);
 
 if (usr->us_status != SLOGIN && usr->us_status != SINIT ||
   usr->us_entry == XDEAD) { 
  deleteuser(usr);
  if (My_mask == 0) { /* if hung up */
   if (usr->us_lvl >= 5)
    daymsg("Hup", "", 0);
 /* KLUDGE AROUND COMPILER BUG */
   kludge = usr->us_con;
   usr->us_con = max(3, kludge - 1); 
   /* Take away con as punishment for hangup */
   mwrite(Allbutme_mask,
    "### %s, the Coward, hung up %s phone\n",
    genuser[My_port].gn_name,
    usr->us_sex == MALE ? "his" : "her");
  }
  writeplayer(usr, usr->us_name, genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_userindex, TRUE); 
 }
 else {
  usr->us_rmcode = 0;
  deleteuser(usr);
  writeusr(usr);
 }
 --nusers;
}
off()
/* Kick off all users in preparation for a crash */
{
 user = usertail;
 while (user != NULL) {
  My_port = user->us_trm;
  My_mask = 1 << My_port;
  logoff(user);
  genuser[My_port].gn_logout = TRUE;
  user = usertail;
 }
}           
do_logout(port)
int  port;
{
 unsigned mask;
 int   i;
 struct  gn_genuser  *gnptr;
 gnptr = &genuser[port];
 mask = 1 << port;
 My_port = port;
 gnptr->gn_ttynum = -1;
 gnptr->gn_usernum = 0;
 gnptr->gn_userindex = 0;
 gnptr->gn_useracct = 0;
 gnptr->gn_logout = FALSE;
 gnptr->gn_tid = 0;
 gnptr->gn_waitmask = 0;
 gnptr->gn_name[0] = '\0';
 All_mask &= ~mask;
 Staff_mask &= ~mask;
}

/*  whydie - enter obituary for player.
  84/08/08.  A. E. Klietz.
 Obituaries are stored in indexes 251 to 253.
The day of the month for the last time the game was brought
up is stored in index 259.  The new obituaries are
stored in indexes 261 to 263.  */
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
/* The following definitions are duplicated in buy.c and update.c */
#define NUM_OBITS   3   /* Number of obituaries in the paper */
#define OLD_OBIT 251 /* Base index for yesterday's obits */
#define NEW_OBIT 261 /* Base index for today's obits */
#define MONTHDAY 259 /* Day-of-month for yesterday's obits */
extern word obit_lvl[NUM_OBITS];
extern char *cname[];
static char *adverb[] = {
"unexpectedly",
"suddenly",
"mysteriously",
"believed to have been",
"reportedly",
};
static char *verb[] = {
"slain",
"killed",
"found dead",
"discovered slain",
"vanquished in combat",
};
whydie()
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register i,j;
 usr = validusr(user);
 /*  Remove player from game */
 usr->us_status = SLOGIN;
 deleteplayer(usr, usr->us_rmcode);
 j = 0;  /* Find lowest level obit */
 for (i=1; i < NUM_OBITS; ++i)
  if (obit_lvl[i] < obit_lvl[j])
   j = i;
 if (obit_lvl[j] < user->us_lvl) { /* If player earns an obit */
  usr->us_entry = XDEAD;  /* "Please enter an obit..." */
  promptuser();
 }
 else
  dead();  /* Standard death handler */
}
enterwhydie()
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register char *string;
 char  clstring[BUFLEN], line1[160];
 register i,j;
 if (strlen(buffer) <= 5) /* If obit isnt long enough */
  return;  /* ask again */
 j=0;  /* Find lowest level obit */
 for (i=1; i < NUM_OBITS; ++i)
  if (obit_lvl[i] < obit_lvl[j])
   j = i;
 if (obit_lvl[j] < usr->us_lvl) { /* Obit confirmed */
  if ((i = strlen(buffer)) > 159)
   buffer[159] = '\0';
  /* Write the obit */
  obit_lvl[j] = usr->us_lvl;
  strncpy(clstring, cname[usr->us_class], 20);
  clstring[0] = toupper(clstring[0]);
  tsprintf(line1, "%.12s, the %.20s level %.20s, was %.30s %.30s yesterday.",
   genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_name, pnth(obit_lvl[j]), clstring,
   adverb[ran(5)-1], verb[ran(5)-1]);
  string = alloc(strlen(line1) + i + 30);
  tsprintf(string, "%2d,%s\n%s\n", obit_lvl[j], line1, buffer);
  writedesc(j + NEW_OBIT, string);
  free(string);
 }  
 else  /* Obit preempted by someone else */
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(2734));
  /* On second thought, forget it!\n */
 dead(); /* Standard death handler */
}

/*  dead - standard death handler.
  84/08/08.  A. E. Klietz.

#include "/std/def.h"

#include "^^def/object.h"

dead()
{
 word  rm;
 register struct ob_object *obj;
 register struct us_user  *usr;
 struct  ob_object *obj2;
 struct  us_user  *body;
 char  string[BUFLEN];
 word  kludge;
 word  lost, i;
 usr = validusr(user);
 rm = usr->us_rmcode;
 
 /* Drop all objects */
 obj = usr->us_objecttail;
 usr->us_objecttail = NULL;
 usr->us_weight = 0;
 usr->us_ac = 10;
 /* Create dead body in room */
 body = alloc(sizeof(struct ob_object));
 tsprintf(string, "body,%.12s's %%s", genuser[usr->us_trm].gn_name);
 body->ob_name = alloc(strlen(string) + 1);
 strcpy(body->ob_name, string);
 body->ob_weight = usr->us_str * 2;
 body->ob_obclass = MISC;
 body->ob_article = NONE;
 
 if (usr->us_con * 5 >= ran(100)) /* If resurrected */
  lost = 1;
 else {
  if (usr->us_lvl >= 5)
   daymsg("Con", "Con failed", usr->us_con + 1);
  mwrite(My_mask, fmt, printdesc(2737, 0, FALSE));
  /* /Your constitution failed./Constitution roll failed./ */
  lost = usr->us_lvl / 2;
 }
 usr->us_usarm = NULL;
 usr->us_usshield = NULL;
 usr->us_usweap = NULL;
 usr->us_entry = XCMD;
 usr->us_status = SNORMAL;
 placeplayer(user, 17);
 usr->us_poisoned = FALSE;
 kludge = usr->us_con;
 usr->us_con = max(3, kludge - lost);
 usr->us_hits = usr->us_maxhits;
 usr->us_fatigue = 5;
 mwrite(My_mask, desc(2735));
 /* \nEverything goes dark... You feel yourself floating away into\n
   Space.  After what seems like an eternity, you faintly hear\n
   Voices!  As you come to your senses, you find.......\n\n\n */
 if (usr->us_lvl > 1) {
  kludge = usr->us_maxhits;
  usr->us_maxhits = max(3, kludge - lost * kludge / usr->us_lvl);
  usr->us_hits = usr->us_maxhits;
  kludge = usr->us_maxfatigue;
  usr->us_maxfatigue = max(3, kludge - lost * kludge / usr->us_lvl);
  kludge = usr->us_maxmagic;

  usr->us_lvl = kludge - lost;

   switch (ran(4)) {
case 1: kludge = usr->us_str;  usr->us_str = max(3, kludge - 1); break;
case 2: kludge = usr->us_int;  usr->us_int = max(3, kludge - 1); break;
case 3: kludge = usr->us_dex;  usr->us_dex = max(3, kludge - 1); break;
case 4: kludge = usr->us_con;  usr->us_con = max(3, kludge - 1); break;
  }
 }

 usr->us_money = max(3, usr->us_pty) * 3;
 usr->us_experience = 0;

/*  Userfile.c - Process user i/o to eperson file.
  84/06/09. A. E. Klietz.
  Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#define  MAX_ENTRIES  7  /* Maximum number of player entries per userindex */
          /* This cannot be raised higher than 7! */
struct ud_user_directory {
 word ud_maxentries;  /* Maximum entries allowed.  0 < x <= MAX_ENTRIES */
 char ud_name[MAX_ENTRIES][12];  /* Name of character entry. */
};
 
static unsigned eperson;  /* File descriptor */
odestroy(object)
struct ob_object *object;
{
 register struct ob_object *cont, *obj;
 cont = object;
 if (cont->ob_obclass == CHEST) 
  while ((obj = cont->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail) != NULL) {
   cont->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail = obj->ob_next;
   odestroy(obj);
  }
 free(cont->ob_name);
 free(cont);
}
  
   
readplayer(player, name, userindex)
struct us_user *player;
char *name;
unsigned userindex;
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register i;
 word  len, indx;
 struct ud_user_directory user_directory;
 register struct ob_object *obj, *obj2;
 word numobjs;
 char *tmpusr;
/* Player indexes are stored in the eperson file in the format
      5 4 3 2 1 0 9 8 7 6 5 4 3 2 1 0
    /    user index            / indx /
*/
 usr = validusr(player);
 if (eperson == 0)
  if ((eperson = random_open("/data/scepter/eperson")) == 0)
   crash(desc(700));
   /* Sce700 - Unable to open eperson file! */
 if (random_read(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory, 
   sizeof(user_directory)) < sizeof(user_directory)) {
  /* If no user directory found. */
  
  usr->us_name[0] = '\0';
  return;
 }
 /* search directory for name match */
 for (i=0; i < MAX_ENTRIES; ++i)
  if (equal(user_directory.ud_name[i], name))
   break;
 if (i >= MAX_ENTRIES) { /* If player not found */
  usr->us_name[0] = '\0';
  return;
 }
  
 indx = i;
 i += (userindex << 3) + 1;
 if ((len = random_allocread(eperson, i, &tmpusr, 5000))
  == 0) { /* if record is zero but directory is not */
   user_directory.ud_name[indx][0] = '\0';  
   random_write(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory, 
    sizeof(user_directory));
   usr->us_name[0] = '\0';
   return;
  }
 copy(usr, tmpusr, sizeof(struct us_user));
 i = sizeof(struct us_user);
 if (i > len)
  crash(desc(702));
  /* Sce702 - user record truncated in eperson! */
 usr->us_weight = 0;
 usr->us_objecttail = NULL;
 usr->us_ac = 10;
 usr->us_next = NULL;
 usr->us_usweap = NULL;
 usr->us_usarm = NULL;
 usr->us_defplayer = NULL;
 usr->us_usshield = NULL;
 usr->us_defmon = NULL;
 usr->us_entry = XNAME;
 usr->us_status = SLOGIN;
 time(&usr->us_lastatk);
 time(&usr->us_lastheal);
 time(&usr->us_drunk);
 usr->us_lastcmd = "";
 if (i < len) { /* if first object */
  obj = alloc(sizeof(struct ob_object));
  numobjs = 1;
  usr->us_objecttail = obj;
  copy(obj, &tmpusr[i], sizeof(struct ob_object));
  usr->us_weight = obj->ob_weight;
  i += sizeof(struct ob_object);
  obj->ob_name = alloc(strlen(&tmpusr[i])+1);
  strncpy(obj->ob_name, &tmpusr[i], BUFLEN);
  i += strlen(&tmpusr[i])+1;
  while (i < len && numobjs < MAXOBJS) { /* if next object */
   obj2 = alloc(sizeof(struct ob_object));
   obj->ob_next = obj2;
   copy(obj2, &tmpusr[i], sizeof(struct ob_object));
   i += sizeof(struct ob_object);
   obj2->ob_name = alloc(strlen(&tmpusr[i])+1);
   strncpy(obj2->ob_name, &tmpusr[i], BUFLEN);
   i += strlen(&tmpusr[i])+1;
   ++numobjs;
   obj = obj2;
   {
    word kludge;
    kludge = usr->us_weight;
    usr->us_weight = kludge + obj->ob_weight;
   }
  }
  obj->ob_next = NULL;
 }
 zero(tmpusr, len);
 free(tmpusr);
}
writeusr(pt)
/* erase user record. */
struct us_user *pt;
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register struct ob_object *object, *object2;
 usr = validusr(pt);
 object = usr->us_objecttail;
 zero(usr, sizeof(struct us_user));
 free(usr);
 if (object != NULL)  /* erase objects */
  do {
   object2 = object->ob_next;
   odestroy(object);
   object = object2;
  } while (object != NULL);
}
boolean writeplayer(player, name, userindex, erase)
/* Write player record.  Return TRUE if successful. */
struct us_user *player;
char *name;
unsigned userindex;
boolean erase;  /* TRUE if record should be freed from memory */
{
 register struct us_user *usr;
 register i;
 register struct ob_object *object;
 word  len, indx;
 struct ud_user_directory user_directory;
 word numobjs;
 char *tmpusr;
 if (erase != TRUE && erase != FALSE)
  crash(desc(593));
  /* Sce593 - writeplayer() erase flag not TRUE or FALSE!\n */
 usr = validusr(player);
 if (random_read(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory, 
   sizeof(user_directory)) < sizeof(user_directory)) {
  /* If no user directory found. */
  
  zero(&user_directory, sizeof(user_directory));
  user_directory.ud_maxentries = 2; /* default */
  if (random_write(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory,
    sizeof(user_directory)) < sizeof(user_directory)) 
   crash(desc(701));
   /* Sce701 - Unable to create new user_directory! */
 
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(702), userindex);
  /* New user directory created for ui=%d.\n */
 }
 /* search directory for name match */
 for (i=0, len = 0; i < MAX_ENTRIES; ++i) {
  if (user_directory.ud_name[i][0] != '\0')
   ++len;
  if (equal(user_directory.ud_name[i], name))
   break;
 }
 if (i >= MAX_ENTRIES) { /* If player not found */
  for (i=0; i < MAX_ENTRIES; ++i) /* search for open slot */
   if (user_directory.ud_name[i][0] == '\0')
    break;
  if (i >= MAX_ENTRIES || len >= user_directory.ud_maxentries) {
   mwrite(My_mask, desc(705), user_directory.ud_maxentries);
   /* You exceeded your stored character limit of %d.\n */
   writeusr(usr);
   return(FALSE);
  }
 }
 indx = i + (userindex << 3) + 1;
 if (usr->us_name[0] == '\0') { /* if record to be deleted */
  random_write(eperson, indx, tmpusr, 0);  /* erase */
  writeusr(usr);  /* free user */
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(706));
  /* Player entry erased. */
  return(TRUE);
 }
 if (!equal(user_directory.ud_name[i], usr->us_name)) {
  strncpy(user_directory.ud_name[i], usr->us_name, 12);
  random_write(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory, 
   sizeof(user_directory));  /* update directory entry */
 }
 tmpusr = alloc(sizeof(struct us_user));
 i = sizeof(struct us_user);
 copy(tmpusr, usr, i);
 if ((object = usr->us_objecttail) != NULL) 
  do {
   word temp, temp2;
   
   if (object->ob_obclass == CHEST) {
    temp2 = object->ob.ob_chest.ob_numinside;
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_numinside = 0;
    temp = object->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail;
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail = NULL;
   }
   free(tmpusr);
   tmpusr = realloc(tmpusr, i + sizeof(struct ob_object)
    + 1 + strlen(object->ob_name));
   copy(&tmpusr[i], object, sizeof(struct ob_object));
   i += sizeof(struct ob_object);
   strncpy(&tmpusr[i], object->ob_name, BUFLEN);
   i += strlen(object->ob_name) + 1;
   if (object->ob_obclass == CHEST) {
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail = temp;
    object->ob.ob_chest.ob_numinside = temp2;
   }
  } while ((object = object->ob_next) != NULL);
 
 if (random_write(eperson, indx, tmpusr,

/*  Roomfile.c - Process room i/o to edunjon file.
  84/06/09. A. E. Klietz.
  Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
#include "^^def/room.h"
#define  ROOMSPERSEG 5 /* 5 rooms per segment */
#define  KLUDGE  /* Kludgily convert rooms to new format */
struct ro_room  room[RMLIMIT];
static unsigned edunjon;
  word slottbl[MAX_PORTS + 1];
static word  active[MAX_PORTS + 1];
        
static  inactivescan = 0;  /* end around search */
static char *tmpdata = NULL;
static word tmploc = 0;
static  word tmpsize = 0;
word s(rm)
/* Return the physical room (0..RMLIMIT) from the logical
   room number (0..4095).  Load in the room if necessary. */
word rm;
{
 register i, segnum;
 if (rm < 0 || rm > 4095)
  crash(desc(710), rm);
  /* Sce710 - Room #%d out of bounds!\n */
 segnum = rm / ROOMSPERSEG;
 i = 0;
 while (i <= MAX_PORTS && slottbl[i] != segnum)
  ++i;
 if (i <= MAX_PORTS) /* if segment found */
  return(i * ROOMSPERSEG + rm - segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
 i = 0;
 while (i <= MAX_PORTS && slottbl[i] > -1)
  ++i;
 if (i > MAX_PORTS) { /* if no open slots */
 /* Use an end-around search for an inactive slot */
  i = inactivescan;
  while (active[i] > 0) {
   if (++i > MAX_PORTS)
    i = 0;
   if (i == inactivescan)
    crash(desc(725));
    /* Sce725 - Virtual room space full!\n */
  }
  inactivescan = (i >= MAX_PORTS) ? 0 : i+1;
 }
 if (slottbl[i] > -1) /* if already occupied */
  writeseg(i, slottbl[i], TRUE);
 readseg(i, segnum);
 return(i * ROOMSPERSEG + rm - segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
}
static writedata(data, len)
/* copy data to tmpdata, and realloc larger as needed */
/* Be sure to set tmpdata = NULL and tmpsize = tmploc = 0 when
   finished using this procedure. */
char *data;
word len;
{
 if (tmpsize == 0) { /* initial allocation */
  tmpdata = alloc(500);
  tmpsize = 500;
 }
  
 while (len > tmpsize - tmploc) { /* allocate larger */
  free(tmpdata);
  tmpdata = realloc(tmpdata, tmpsize + 500);
  tmpsize += 500;
 }
 copy(&tmpdata[tmploc], data, len);
 tmploc += len;
}
static boolean readdata(data, len)
/* copy tmpdata to data */
/* Be sure to read tmpdata and set tmpsize = size, and tmploc = 0
   BEFORE using this procedure.  Free tmpdata afterwards and set
   tmpsize = tmploc = 0 */
char *data;
word len;
{
 copy(data, &tmpdata[tmploc], min(len, tmpsize - tmploc));
 tmploc = min(tmpsize, tmploc + len);
 return(tmploc >= tmpsize);
}
writeseg(slotnum, segnum, erase)
/* write out room segment, optionally erase room data */
word slotnum, segnum;
boolean erase;
{
 word rm, r;
 struct mo_monster *monster, *mon2;
  register struct ob_object *object,  *obj2;
 if (segnum < 0 || segnum > 4095 / ROOMSPERSEG) {
  error_handler(0);
  crash(desc(730), segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
  /* Sce730 - Room cluster %d out of bounds! */
 }
 
 for (r=0; r < ROOMSPERSEG; ++r) {
  word k;
  rm = r + ROOMSPERSEG * slotnum;
  for (k=0; k < 7; ++k)
   if (((unsigned) room[rm].ro_adjoin[k]) > 4095) {
    error_handler(0);
    crash(desc(544), r + ROOMSPERSEG * segnum);
    /* Sce544 - Room #%d seems to be trashed.\n */
   }
#ifdef  KLUDGE
  room[rm].ro_version = 1;
#endif
  writedata(&room[rm], sizeof(struct ro_room));
  object = room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail;
  while (object != NULL) {
   writedata(object, sizeof(struct ob_object));
   writedata(object->ob_name, strlen(object->ob_name)+1);
   savechest(object, erase);
   obj2 = object->ob_next;
   if (erase) {
    free(object->ob_name);
    free(object);
   }
   object = obj2;
  }
 
  monster = room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail;
  while (monster != NULL) {
   monster->mo_defplayer = NULL;
   writedata(monster, sizeof(struct mo_monster));
   writedata(monster->mo_name, strlen(monster->mo_name)+1);
   object = monster->mo_objecttail;
   while (object != NULL) {
    writedata(object, sizeof(struct ob_object));
    writedata(object->ob_name, strlen(object->ob_name)+1);
    obj2 = object->ob_next;
    if (erase) {
     free(object->ob_name);
     free(object);
    }
    object = obj2;
   }
   mon2 = monster->mo_next;
   if (erase) {
    free(monster->mo_name);
    free(monster);
   }
   monster = mon2;
  }
 }
 if (random_write(edunjon, segnum, tmpdata, tmploc) != tmploc) {
  error_handler(0);
  crash(desc(731), segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
  /* Sce731 - Unable to write cluster %d!\n */
 }
 free(tmpdata);
 tmpdata = NULL;
 tmpsize = tmploc = 0;
}
static savechest(cont, erase)
struct ob_object *cont;
boolean erase;
{
 register struct ob_object *obj, *obj2;
 if (cont->ob_obclass == CHEST) {
  obj = cont->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail;
  while (obj != NULL) {
   writedata(obj, sizeof(struct ob_object));
   writedata(obj->ob_name, strlen(obj->ob_name)+1);
   savechest(obj, erase);
   obj2 = obj->ob_next;
   if (erase) {
    free(obj->ob_name);
    free(obj);
   }
   obj = obj2;
  }
 }
}
static readname(ptrptr)
char **ptrptr;
{
 *ptrptr = alloc(strlen(&tmpdata[tmploc])+1);
 readdata(*ptrptr, strlen(&tmpdata[tmploc])+1);
}
static word object_size;  
readseg(slotnum, segnum)
word slotnum, segnum;
{
 word r, rm;
 register struct ob_object *object, *object2;
 struct mo_monster *monster,*monster2;
#ifdef  KLUDGE
 boolean newrooms;     /* KLUDGE */
#endif
 object_size = sizeof(struct ob_object);
 if (segnum < 0 || segnum > 4095 / ROOMSPERSEG)
  crash(desc(730), segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
  /* Sce730 - Room cluster %d out of bounds!\n */
 if (edunjon == 0)
  if ((edunjon = random_open("/data/scepter/edunjon")) == 0)
   crash(desc(737));
   /* Sce737 - Unable to open edunjon!\n */
 slottbl[slotnum] = segnum;
 tmploc = 0;
#ifdef  KLUDGE
 newrooms = FALSE;
#endif
 if ((tmpsize = random_allocread(edunjon, segnum, &tmpdata, 5000))
   < sizeof(struct ro_room)) {
#ifdef KLUDGE
  newrooms = TRUE;
#endif
  tmpdata  = alloc(tmpsize = sizeof(struct ro_room) * ROOMSPERSEG);
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(732), segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
  /* New room cluster %d allocated.\n"); */
 }
  
 for (r = 0; r < ROOMSPERSEG; ++r) {
  rm = r + ROOMSPERSEG * slotnum;
  readdata(&room[rm], sizeof(struct ro_room));
  room[rm].ro_rmplayertail = NULL;
#ifdef  KLUDGE
  if (newrooms)
   room[rm].ro_version = 1;  /* KLUDGE */
  if (room[rm].ro_version != 1)
   object_size = sizeof(struct ob_object) + 6;
#endif
  if (room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail != NULL) {
   object = alloc(object_size);
   room[rm].ro_rmobjecttail = object;
   readdata(object, object_size);
   readname(&object->ob_name);
   getchest(object);
   while (object->ob_next != NULL) {
    object2 = alloc(object_size);
    object->ob_next = object2;
    readdata(object2, object_size);
    readname(&object2->ob_name);
    object = object2;
    getchest(object);
   }
  }
  
  if (room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail != NULL) {
   monster = alloc(sizeof(struct mo_monster));
   room[rm].ro_rmmonstertail = monster;
   readdata(monster, sizeof(struct mo_monster));
   readname(&monster->mo_name);
   monster->mo_defplayer = NULL;
   getmonobjects(monster);
   while (monster->mo_next != NULL) {
    monster2 = alloc(sizeof(struct mo_monster));
    monster->mo_next = monster2;
    readdata(monster2, sizeof(struct mo_monster));
    readname(&monster2->mo_name);
    monster2->mo_defplayer = NULL;
    getmonobjects(monster2);
    monster = monster2;
   }
  }
 }
 if (!readdata(monster, 0)) /* if not EOF */
  crash(desc(736), segnum * ROOMSPERSEG);
  /* Sce736 - Failed to read cluster %d entirely!\n */
 free(tmpdata);
 tmpsize = tmploc = 0;
}
static getchest(cont)
struct ob_object *cont;
{
 register struct ob_object *obj, *obj2;
 if (cont->ob_obclass == CHEST) 
  if (cont->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail != NULL) {
   obj = alloc(object_size);
   cont->ob.ob_chest.ob_objecttail = obj;
   readdata(obj, object_size);
   readname(&obj->ob_name);
   getchest(obj);
   while (obj->ob_next != NULL) {
    obj2 = alloc(object_size);
    obj->ob_next = obj2;
    readdata(obj2, object_size);
    readname(&obj2->ob_name);
    obj = obj2;
    getchest(obj);
   }
  }
}
static getmonobjects(monster)
struct mo_monster *monster;
{
 register struct ob_object *obj, *obj2;
 if (monster->mo_objecttail != NULL) {
  obj = alloc(object_size);
  monster->mo_objecttail = obj;
  readdata(obj, object_size);
  readname(&obj->ob_name);
  while (obj->ob_next != NULL) {
   obj2 = alloc(object_size);
   obj->ob_next = obj2;
   readdata(obj2, object_size);
   readname(&obj2->ob_name);
   obj = obj2;
  }
 }
}
word sa(rm)
/* Load and set active-room segment */
word rm;
{
 register seg, segrm;
 segrm = s(rm);
 seg = segrm / ROOMSPERSEG;
 ++(active[seg]);
 return(segrm);
}
inactive(rm)
/* Set room inactive */
word rm;
{
 register seg;
 seg = rm / ROOMSPERSEG;
 if (active[seg] <= 0)
  crash(desc(765));
  /* Sce765 - Non active segment assumed active! */
  
 --(active[seg]);               
}
word w(rm)
/* Return logical number (0-4095) of physical room (0..RMLIMIT) */
word rm;
{
 register where;
 if (rm < 0 || rm >= RMLIMIT)
  return(0);
 where = slottbl[rm / ROOMSPERSEG] * ROOMSPERSEG + rm
   - (rm / ROOMSPERSEG) * ROOMSPERSEG;
 if (where < 0 || where >= 4096)
  where = 0;
 return(where);
}

/*  elist - I/O for OLIST and MLIST.
  84/08/02. A. E. Klietz.
  Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
unsigned elist;
static char *thing = "thing";
struct ob_object *get_olist(n)
/*  Return nth object in olist */
{
 register struct ob_object *obj;
 char *buf, *string;
 obj = alloc(sizeof(struct ob_object));
 if (n <= 0 || n > 4095 || 
   random_allocread(elist, n+10000, &buf, 5000)
  < sizeof(struct ob_object)) { /* If no object */
  string = thing;
  obj->ob_name = alloc(strlen(string)+1);
  strncpy(obj->ob_name, string, BUFLEN);
  obj->ob_obclass = MISC;
  obj->ob_carry = TRUE;
  obj->ob_weight = 1;
  obj->ob_version = 1;
 }
 else {
  word obj_size;
  obj_size = sizeof(struct ob_object);
  copy(obj, buf, sizeof(struct ob_object));
  if (obj->ob_version != 1)
   obj_size += 6;
  obj->ob_name = alloc(strlen(&buf[obj_size])+1);
  strncpy(obj->ob_name, &buf[obj_size], BUFLEN);
  free(buf);
 }
 obj->ob_next = NULL;
 return(obj);
}
struct mo_monster *get_mlist(n)
/*  Return nth monster in mlist */
{
 register struct mo_monster *mon;
 char *buf, *string;
 mon = alloc(sizeof(struct mo_monster));
 if (n <= 0 || n > 4095 || 
   random_allocread(elist, n+20000, &buf, 5000)
   < sizeof(struct mo_monster)) { /* If no monster */
  string = thing;
  mon->mo_name = alloc(strlen(string)+1);
  strncpy(mon->mo_name, string, BUFLEN);
  mon->mo_defend = TRUE;
  mon->mo_atklastaggr = TRUE;
  mon->mo_lvl = 1;
  mon->mo_hits = 10;
  mon->mo_maxhits = 10;
  mon->mo_top = TRUE;
  mon->mo_num = 1;
 }
 else {
  copy(mon, buf, sizeof(struct mo_monster));
  mon->mo_name = alloc(strlen(&buf[sizeof(struct mo_monster)])+1);
  strncpy(mon->mo_name, &buf[sizeof(struct mo_monster)], BUFLEN);
  free(buf);
 }
 mon->mo_next = NULL;
 return(mon);
}

struct ob_object *obj;
word n;
{
 register char *buf;
 word  len;
 if (n < 0 || n > 4095) {
  mwrite(My_mask, desc(1230));
  /* Olist write out of bounds.\n */
  return;
 }
 if (equal(obj->ob_name, thing)) {
  random_write(elist, n+10000, 0, 0);
  return;
 }
 obj->ob_version = 1;
 buf = alloc(len = sizeof(struct ob_object) + strlen(obj->ob_name) + 1);
 copy(buf, obj, sizeof(struct ob_object));
 strncpy(&buf[sizeof(struct ob_object)], obj->ob_name, BUFLEN);
 if (random_write(elist, n+10000, buf, len) != len)
  crash(desc(1240), n);
  /* Sce1240 - Failed to write olist at %d!\n */
 free(buf);
}

/*  random_read - perform read of random file.
  84/04/15.     A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
#include "random_msgs.h"
word random_read(fp, index, data, maxsize)
/* Read record from random file at the given index.  Up to 'maxsize'
bytes are read.  The actual number of bytes read is returned. 
KLUDGE: 
 'data' is allocated from free memory to fit the correct size
 And top few words are reserved for ra_readmsg_reply information */
 
unsigned_word fp;
unsigned_word   index;
char   **data; /* Pointer to character string pointer */
unsigned_word maxsize;
{
 register struct  rf_random_file  *filedesc;
 register struct  np_node_pointer  *node_pointer;
 struct  rf_random_file *get_rf_index();
 word  hash, hash_index(), finalsize;
 boolean  read_node();
 struct  n_node    node;
 if ((filedesc = get_rf_index(fp)) == NULL_RANDOM_FILE)
  /* If no such fp found */
  return(0);  /* no bytes read. */
 hash = hash_index(index, HASHSIZE);
 /* Generate hash bucket for index */
 node_pointer = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash];  /* head node in bucket */
 while (node_pointer != NULL_NODE_POINTER && 
    node_pointer->np_data.np_index != index) 
  /* scan links in bucket for a match */
  node_pointer = node_pointer->np_next;
 if (node_pointer == NULL_NODE_POINTER) /* if no such index */
  return(0);  
 if ((finalsize = read_cache(filedesc, index, maxsize, data)) != 0) 
  return(finalsize);  /* if found in cache */
 if (!read_node(filedesc, node_pointer->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
  return(0);  /* if bad node read */
 if (node.n_allocflag >= 0 || /* if not allocated */
  node.n_index != index || /* if index mismatch */
  node.n_checkword != N_CHECK) { /* if bad check bits */
  errlog("warn:random_read - bad node in %s at %d (index %d).\n",
   filedesc->rf_name, (word) node_pointer->np_offset, index);
  return(0);
 }
                          
 *data = alloc(node.n_data_size + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
 if ((finalsize = fget(*data + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply),
   node.n_data_size, filedesc->rf_fp))
  != node.n_data_size)
  errlog("warn:random_read - truncated read. in %s at %d (index %d).\n",
   filedesc->rf_name, (word) node_pointer->np_offset, index);
 else {
  add_cache(filedesc, index, finalsize, *data + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
  if (node.n_data_size > maxsize) { /* if too large */
   free(*data);
   *data = realloc(*data, maxsize + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
   finalsize = maxsize;
  }
 }
 return(finalsize);
}
struct rf_random_file *get_rf_index(fp)
/* Return a pointer to file descriptor table based on the fp */
unsigned_word fp;
{
 if (fp <= 0 || fp > MAXFILES)
 /* if not active file */
  return(NULL_RANDOM_FILE);
 else {
  if (filelist[fp-1].rf_fp == 0)
   return(NULL_RANDOM_FILE);
  else
   return(&filelist[fp-1]);
 }
}
word hash_index(index, hashsize)
/* Compress an arbitrary index down into the range [0..HASHSIZE-1]
   hashsize should be a prime number for best results. */
unsigned_word index, hashsize;
{
 return(abs(index % hashsize));
}
