/*  random_write - perform write of random file.
  84/04/15.     A. E. Klietz.
 
        CopyRight (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.   All Rights Reserved.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
word random_write(fp, index, data, size)
/* Write a record to a random file at the given index.  'size'
bytes are written.  The actual number of bytes written is returned. */
unsigned_word fp;
unsigned_word index;
char   *data;
unsigned_word size;
{
 register struct rf_random_file  *filedesc;
 struct  rf_random_file  *get_rf_index();
 word  hash, finalsize,  hash_index();
 register struct  np_node_pointer  *node_pointer;
 struct  np_node_pointer  *alloc_node(), *realloc_node();
 struct  n_node    node;
 boolean  read_node(), write_node();
 if ((filedesc = get_rf_index(fp)) == NULL_RANDOM_FILE)
  /* If no such fp found */
  return(0);  /* no bytes read. */
 if (size < 0)
  size == 0;
 else
  if (size + NODE_SIZE > N_MAXSIZE) /* if node too large */
   size = N_MAXSIZE - NODE_SIZE;
 hash = hash_index(index, HASHSIZE);
 /* Generate hash bucket for index */
 node_pointer = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash];  /* head node in bucket */
 while (node_pointer != NULL_NODE_POINTER && 
    node_pointer->np_data.np_index != index) 
  /* scan links in bucket for a match */
  node_pointer = node_pointer->np_next;
 if (node_pointer == NULL_NODE_POINTER && size > 0) { /* if no such index */
  if ((node_pointer = alloc_node(filedesc, index,
    size + NODE_SIZE, &node.n_size)) == NULL_NODE_POINTER)
   /* if unable to allocate new node */
   return(0);
 }
 else {
  remove_cache(filedesc, index);  /* remove from cache */
  if (size > 0) { /* if not to delete */
   if (!read_node(filedesc, node_pointer->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
    return(0); /* if node read */
   if (size + NODE_SIZE > node.n_size || 
     node.n_size > size + NODE_SIZE + N_MINSIZE)
    /* if data too big or shrunk enough to spawn empty node */
       if ((node_pointer = realloc_node(filedesc, index, size + 
                   NODE_SIZE, node_pointer, &node.n_size)) == NULL_NODE_POINTER)
     /* if unable to allocate new node */
     return(0);
  }
  else /* node to be deleted */
   if (node_pointer != NULL_NODE_POINTER) { /* if node exists */
    free_node(filedesc, index, node_pointer); /* release node */
                rewind(filedesc->rf_fp);   /* KLUDGE - flush output to disk */
    return(0);
   } 
   else
    return(0); /* tried to free nonexistant node */
 }
 node.n_checkword = N_CHECK;  /* Construct and write a node header */
 node.n_allocflag = -1;
 node.n_index = index; 
 node.n_data_size = size;
 if (!write_node(filedesc, node_pointer->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
  /* if bad write */
  return(0);
 if ((finalsize = fput(data, size, filedesc->rf_fp)) != size) {
   /* if fput failed */
  errlog("warn:random_write - write failed in %s at %d (index %d).\n",
  filedesc->rf_name, (word) node_pointer->np_offset, index);
  return(0);
 }
 rewind(filedesc->rf_fp); /* KLUDGE - flush data to disk - KLUDGE */
 return(finalsize);
}
struct np_node_pointer *alloc_node(filedesc, index, size, actual_size)
/* Search the AV list for a node of at least 'size' bytes using an
end-around first fit algorithm.  Grow file if not found
otherwise shrink the node in the AV list
(or unlink it if its too small) and alloc and insert the new node in the
filedesc->rf_hashtbl[].   Return actual_size. */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word index;
unsigned_word size;
word *actual_size;
{
 register struct np_node_pointer *new_node_ptr, *allocp, *alloc_trailer;
 struct  np_node_pointer  *old_allocp;
 struct n_node node;
 boolean read_node(), write_node();
 word hash;
 longword offset;
 
 old_allocp = alloc_trailer = allocp = filedesc->rf_allocp;
 for (allocp = allocp->np_next; ; 
   alloc_trailer = allocp, allocp = allocp->np_next) {
  if (allocp->np_data.np_sizenode >= size) { /* if node fits */
   /* create node and insert in hash table */
   new_node_ptr = alloc(sizeof(struct np_node_pointer));
   copy(new_node_ptr, allocp, sizeof(struct np_node_pointer));
   new_node_ptr->np_data.np_index = index;
   hash = hash_index(index, HASHSIZE);
   new_node_ptr->np_next = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash];
   filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash] = new_node_ptr;
   if (allocp->np_data.np_sizenode > size + N_MINSIZE) { 
    /* if node can be split */
    new_node_ptr->np_offset += allocp->np_data.np_sizenode - size;
    *actual_size = size;
#ifdef PARANOID
    if (!read_node(filedesc, allocp->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
     return(NULL_NODE_POINTER); /* if bad read */
#else
    node.n_checkword = N_CHECK;
    node.n_index = index;
#endif
    allocp->np_data.np_sizenode -= size;  /* shrink size */
    node.n_size = allocp->np_data.np_sizenode;   
    node.n_allocflag = 0;
    node.n_data_size = 0;
    if (!write_node(filedesc, allocp->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
     return(NULL_NODE_POINTER); /* if bad write */
   }     
   else { /* delete node from free list */ 
    *actual_size = allocp->np_data.np_sizenode;
    alloc_trailer->np_next = allocp->np_next;
    free(allocp);
    allocp = alloc_trailer->np_next;
   }
   break;
  }
  if (allocp == old_allocp) { /* if no space found */
   filedesc->rf_allocp = allocp; /* update allocp */
   more_filespace(filedesc);  /* get more file space */
   allocp = old_allocp = alloc_trailer = filedesc->rf_allocp;
  }
 }  
 filedesc->rf_allocp = allocp;  /* update alloc pointer */
 return(new_node_ptr);
}
  
more_filespace(filedesc)
/* Get more file space. */
struct  rf_random_file  *filedesc;
{
 word i;
 char  *zeros;
 register struct np_node_pointer *new_node_ptr;
 struct rb_root_block root_block;
 struct n_node node;
 boolean write_node();
 rewind(filedesc->rf_fp);
 if (fget(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), filedesc->rf_fp)
  != sizeof(root_block)) 
  crash("fatal:more_filespace - unable to read root block!\n");
 /* insert new space in hash table and then free_node() it. */
 new_node_ptr = alloc(sizeof(struct np_node_pointer));
 i = hash_index(0, HASHSIZE);
 new_node_ptr->np_next = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i];
 filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i] = new_node_ptr;
 new_node_ptr->np_offset = root_block.rb_filesize; 
 new_node_ptr->np_data.np_index = 0;
 root_block.rb_corrupt = 1; /* flag file as corrupt */
 root_block.rb_filesize += SIZE_CHUNK * NUM_CHUNKS;
 rewind(filedesc->rf_fp);
 fput(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), filedesc->rf_fp);
 fseek(filedesc->rf_fp, new_node_ptr->np_offset, 0);
 zeros = alloc(SIZE_CHUNK); /* divide into chunks */  
 for (i=0; i < NUM_CHUNKS; ++i) 
  if (fput(zeros, SIZE_CHUNK, filedesc->rf_fp) != SIZE_CHUNK)
   crash("fatal:more_filespace - fput error while increasing size of %s.\n",
    filedesc->rf_name);
 free(zeros);
 
 node.n_checkword = N_CHECK;
 node.n_allocflag = 0;
 node.n_index = 0;
 node.n_size = NUM_CHUNKS * SIZE_CHUNK;
 node.n_data_size = 0;
 write_node(filedesc, new_node_ptr->np_offset, TRUE, &node);
 fclose(filedesc->rf_fp);  /* close and reopen file in order to update
         directory so file will not be corrupted
          if the system should crash. */
 if ((filedesc->rf_fp = fopen(filedesc->rf_name, "rwv")) == 0)
  /* if cannot reopen file */
  crash("alloc:fatal - cannot reopen file %s!\n",
   filedesc->rf_name);       
 i = 0; /* mark file as restored */
 fput(&i, sizeof(word), filedesc->rf_fp);
    free_node(filedesc, 0, new_node_ptr);  /* link node into free list */
}
free_node(filedesc, index, node_ptr)
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word   index;
struct np_node_pointer *node_ptr;
{
 word hash;
 register struct np_node_pointer *temp_node_ptr;
 struct n_node node, othernode;
 longword offset;
 boolean  remove_allocnode(), read_node(), write_node();
 if (index != node_ptr->np_data.np_index) {
  errlog("warn:free_node - index mismatch. %d - %d.\n", index,
   node_ptr->np_data.np_index);
  return;
 }
 if (remove_allocnode(filedesc, index, node_ptr) == FALSE)
  return;
 /* insert in free list */
 node_ptr->np_next = filedesc->rf_AV->np_next;
 filedesc->rf_AV->np_next = node_ptr;
 if (!read_node(filedesc, node_ptr->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
  return;
 node.n_data_size = 0;
 node.n_allocflag = 0;
 node_ptr->np_data.np_sizenode = node.n_size;
#ifndef PARANOID
 fseek(filedesc->rf_fp, node.n_size - NODE_SIZE, 1);
#endif
 for (;;) { /* check adjacent node and merge if possible */
  
#ifndef PARANOID
  if (fget(&othernode, NODE_SIZE, filedesc->rf_fp) != NODE_SIZE) 
#else
  if (!read_node(filedesc, node_ptr->np_offset + node.n_size,
    FALSE, &othernode)) /* if past  end of file */
#endif
   break;
   
  if (othernode.n_allocflag < 0) /* if allocated */
   break; /* stop merging */ 
  node.n_size += othernode.n_size; /* merge */
#ifndef PARANOID
  fseek(filedesc->rf_fp, othernode.n_size - NODE_SIZE, 1);
#endif
 }
 
 if (!write_node(filedesc, node_ptr->np_offset, TRUE, &node))
  return;
}
struct np_node_pointer *realloc_node(filedesc, index, size, node_pointer, actual_size)
/* reallocate a node to a new size. */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word index;
unsigned_word   size;
struct np_node_pointer *node_pointer;
unsigned_word   *actual_size;
{
 struct np_node_pointer *alloc_node();
 free_node(filedesc, index, node_pointer);
 return(alloc_node(filedesc, index, size, actual_size));
}
boolean read_node(filedesc, offset, err_flag, node)
/* read node at absolute offset.
TRUE is returned if the read is successful */
struct  rf_random_file *filedesc;
longword offset;
boolean err_flag;
struct n_node *node;
{
 if (fseek(filedesc->rf_fp, offset, 0) < 0) {
 /* if seek failed */
  if (err_flag)
   errlog("warn:read_node - seek failed for %s to %d.\n",
    filedesc->rf_name, (word) offset);
  return(FALSE);
 }
 if (fget(node, NODE_SIZE, filedesc->rf_fp) != NODE_SIZE) {
  if (err_flag)
   errlog("warn:read_node - node fget failed in %s (offset %d)\n",
    filedesc->rf_name, (word) offset); 
  return(FALSE);
 }
 if (node->n_checkword != N_CHECK) {
  errlog("warn:read_node - bad checkword at %d in %s.\n",
   (word) offset, filedesc->rf_name);
  return(FALSE);
 }
 return(TRUE);
}
boolean write_node(filedesc, offset, err_flag, node)
/* write node at absolute offset.  
TRUE is returned if the write is successful */
struct  rf_random_file *filedesc;
longword offset; 
boolean err_flag;
struct n_node *node;
{
 if (fseek(filedesc->rf_fp, offset, 0) < 0) {
 /* if seek failed */
        if (err_flag)
   errlog("warn:write_node - seek failed for %s to %d.\n",
    filedesc->rf_name, (word) offset);
  return(FALSE);
 }
 if (fput(node, NODE_SIZE, filedesc->rf_fp) != NODE_SIZE) {
  if (err_flag)
   errlog("warn:write_node - node fget failed in %s (offset %d)\n",
    filedesc->rf_name, (word) offset); 
  return(FALSE);
 }
 return(TRUE);
}
boolean remove_allocnode(filedesc, index, node_ptr)
/* remove the given node from the allocated node hash table. */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word index;
struct np_node_pointer *node_ptr;
{
 word hash;
 register struct np_node_pointer *temp_node_ptr;
 /* Remove node from hash table */
 hash = hash_index(index, HASHSIZE);  
 temp_node_ptr = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash];
 if (temp_node_ptr == NULL_NODE_POINTER) {
  errlog("warn:free_node - attempted to free empty hash bucket at %d\n",
   index);
  return(FALSE);
 }
 if (temp_node_ptr == node_ptr)
  filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash] = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash]->np_next;
 else { 
  while (temp_node_ptr->np_next != NULL_NODE_POINTER
    && temp_node_ptr->np_next != node_ptr)
    temp_node_ptr = temp_node_ptr->np_next;
  if (temp_node_ptr->np_next == NULL_NODE_POINTER) {
   errlog("warn:free_node - unable to delete index %d.\n", index);
   return(FALSE);
  }
  temp_node_ptr->np_next = node_ptr->np_next;
 }
 return(TRUE);
}
hash bucket at %d\n",
   index);

/*  random_create - Create a random file.
  84/04/21.     A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
word random_create(filename, initsize, maxcachesize)
/* create a random file starting with *size* words.  Must be
   at least 1024. */
char *filename;
word initsize, maxcachesize;
{
 struct rb_root_block root_block;
 struct n_node   node;
 char *zeros;
 FILE *fp;
 if (initsize < 1024)
  return(0);
 if (maxcachesize < 0 || maxcachesize > 16384)
  return(0);
 fdelete(filename);
 if ((fp = fopen(filename, "wv")) == 0)
  return(0);
 zero(&root_block, sizeof(root_block));
 root_block.rb_corrupt = 0; 
 root_block.rb_checkword = N_CHECK;
 root_block.rb_filesize = initsize;
 root_block.rb_maxcachesize = maxcachesize;
 fput(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), fp);
 initsize -= sizeof(root_block);
 zero(&node, sizeof(node));
 node.n_checkword = N_CHECK;
 node.n_allocflag = 0;
 node.n_index = 0;
 node.n_size = initsize;
 node.n_data_size = 0;
 
 fput(&node, sizeof(node), fp);
 
 zeros = alloc(initsize - sizeof(node)); 
 fput(zeros, initsize - sizeof(node), fp);
 free(zeros);
 fclose(fp);
 return(random_open(filename));
}

/*  random_open - Open a random file.
  84/04/20.     A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
struct rf_random_file filelist[MAXFILES];
word random_open(filename)
char *filename;
{
 register i;
 for (i=0; i < MAXFILES; ++i) 
  if (filelist[i].rf_fp == 0)
   break;
 if (i >= MAXFILES) {
  errlog("warn:random_open - too many open files.\n");
  return(0);
 }
    if (random_fpopen(&filelist[i], filename) == 0)
  return(0);
 else
  return(i+1);
}
word random_fpopen(filedesc, filename)
/* Open random file.  Return nonzero if successful */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
char *filename;
{
 FILE  *fp;
 struct  rb_root_block root_block;
 struct  n_node   node;
 longword filesize;
 word hash, i;
 register struct  np_node_pointer *node_pointer;
 zero (filedesc, sizeof(struct rf_random_file));
 if ((fp = fopen(filename, "rwv")) == 0) /* if cannot open */  
  return(0);
 if (fget(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), fp) != sizeof(root_block)) {
  errlog("warn:random_open - unable to read root block of %s.\n",
   filename);
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 if (root_block.rb_checkword != N_CHECK) {
  errlog("warn:random_open - bad checkword on %s.\n", filename);
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 if (root_block.rb_corrupt != 0) {
  errlog("warn:random_open - %s is corrupt!\n",
   filename);
  fclose(fp);
  return(0);
 }
 filesize = sizeof(root_block);
 /* Set up AV node */
 node_pointer = alloc(sizeof(struct np_node_pointer));
 filedesc->rf_AV = node_pointer;
 node_pointer->np_next = node_pointer;
 node_pointer->np_offset = (word) &root_block.rb_AVnode - 
  (word) &root_block;
 node_pointer->np_data.np_sizenode = 0;
 filedesc->rf_fp = fp;
 filedesc->rf_maxcachesize = root_block.rb_maxcachesize;
 strcpy(filedesc->rf_name, filename);
 while (filesize < root_block.rb_filesize) {
  if (fget(&node, NODE_SIZE, filedesc->rf_fp) != NODE_SIZE) {
   errlog("warn:random_open - bad node read at %04x in %s.\n",
    (word) filesize, filename);
   fclose(fp);
   filedesc->rf_fp = 0;
   return(0);
  }  
  if (node.n_checkword != N_CHECK) { 
   errlog("warn:random_open - bad checkword at %04x in %s.\n",
    (word) filesize, filename);
   fclose(fp);
    filedesc->rf_fp = 0;
    return(0);
  }
  
  node_pointer = alloc(sizeof(struct np_node_pointer));    
  node_pointer->np_offset = filesize;
  if (node.n_size < sizeof(node) || node.n_size > N_MAXSIZE) {
   errlog("warn:random_open - bad node size of %d in %s at %04x.\n",
    node.n_size, filename, (word) filesize);
   fclose(fp);
   filedesc->rf_fp = 0;
   return(0);
  }
  if (node.n_allocflag < 0) { /* if allocated */
   /* insert in hash table */
      hash = hash_index(node.n_index, HASHSIZE);
   node_pointer->np_next = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash];
   filedesc->rf_hashtbl[hash] = node_pointer;
   node_pointer->np_data.np_index = node.n_index;
  }
  else { /* insert in link table */
   node_pointer->np_next = filedesc->rf_AV->np_next; 
   filedesc->rf_AV->np_next = node_pointer;
   node_pointer->np_data.np_sizenode = node.n_size;
  };
 
  fseek(fp, (longword) (node.n_size - sizeof(node)), 1);
  filesize += node.n_size;
 }
 if (fget(&i, 1, fp) > 0) { /* if more data */
  errlog("warn:random_open - more data beyond end of file %s.\n",
   filename);
  fclose(fp);
  filedesc->rf_fp = 0;
  return(0);
 }
 filedesc->rf_allocp = filedesc->rf_AV;
 rewind(fp);   /* KLUDGE - flush data to disk???  KLUDGE */
 return(fp); 
}
desc->rf_AV->np_next; 
   filedesc->rf_AV->np_next = node_pointer;
   node_pointer->np_data.np_sizenode = node.n_size;
  };

/*  random_edit - edit a random file.
  84/06/06 A. E. Klietz.
  Copyright (c) 1984.  Alan E. Klietz.  
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
main()
{
 char buffer[256], wrd[256], wrd2[256], fbuf[256];
 char *data;
 register i, fp, j, k;
 unbreakable();
 setup("random_test");
 set_handler();
 setexit();
 for (;;) {
  breakable();
  print("random_edit? ");
  if (readline(buffer) == 0)
   continue;
  reset_parse();
  parse(buffer, wrd);
  if (subset(wrd, "end"))
   exit(0);
  if (subset(wrd, "read")) {
   parse(buffer, wrd);
   i = atoi(wrd);
   if (i == 0) {
    print("Missing record number.\n");
    continue;
   }
   print("Reading record %d.\n",i);
   if (random_read(fp, i, fbuf, 255) <= 0)   
    print("Read failed.\n");
   else {
    fbuf[255] = '\0';
    print("%s\n", fbuf);
   }
   continue;
  }
        if (subset(wrd, "write")) {
   parse(buffer, wrd);
   i = atoi(wrd);
   if (i == 0) {
    print("Missing record number.\n");
    continue;
   }
   print("Enter text for record %d\n? ", i);
   reset_parse();
   readline_wide(buffer, 255);
   j = strlen(buffer) + 1;
   for (k=0; k < j-1; ++k)
    if (buffer[k] == '\\' && buffer[k+1] == 'n') {
     buffer[k] = '\n';
     strcpy(&buffer[k+1], &buffer[k+2]);
     --j;
    }
     
   if (j <= 1) {
    j = 0;
    print("Erasing line.. ");
   }
   if (random_write(fp, i, buffer, j) != j)
    print("Write failed.\n");
   else
    print("Write ok.\n");
   continue;
  }
  if (subset(wrd, "open")) {
   if (parse(buffer, wrd) <= 0) {
    print("Missing name of file to open.\n");
    continue;
   }
   if ((fp = random_open(wrd)) == 0)
    print("open failed.\n");
   else
    print("file opened.\n");
   continue;
  }
  if (subset(wrd, "close")) {
   random_close(fp);
   print("Random file closed.\n");
   continue;
  }
  if (subset(wrd, "create")) {
   parse(buffer, wrd);
   parse(buffer, wrd2);
   i = atoi(wrd2);
   parse(buffer, wrd2);
   j = atoi(wrd2);
   if ((fp = random_create(wrd, i, j)) == 0)
    print("create failed.\n");
   else
    print("%s created.\n", wrd);
   continue;
  }
  if (subset(wrd, "compact")) {
   random_compact(fp);
   print("File compacted.\n");
   continue;
  }
        if (subset(wrd, "list")) {
           parse(buffer,wrd);
           i = atoi(wrd);
              if(i == 0) {
                 print ("Missing list start.\n");
                 continue;
              }
           parse(buffer,wrd);
           j = atoi(wrd);
              if(j == 0 || j < i) {
                 print ("Bad list end.\n");
                 continue;
              }
           for (;i < j+1;++i)
              if(random_read(fp,i,fbuf,255)>0)
                 print ("%d: %s\n",i,fbuf);
         }
  if (subset(wrd, "help")) {
   print("Edit commands are:\n");
            print ("Close Compact End Help List Open Read Write. \n\n");
   continue;
  }
 }
}
                 continue;
              }
           parse(buffer,wrd);
           j = atoi(wrd);
              if(j == 0 || j < i) {
                 print ("Bad list end.\n");
                 continue;

RFM comes in two parts:
  /std/random_file.o is a set of subroutines that are linked into the
parent program. 
  /std/random_msgs is a child program that handles the actual file i/o.
It is implemented with shared code space.
link_msgs - link together the /std/random_msgs program.
random_cache.c - handles cache mechanism
random_close - closes RFM file and flushes cache.
random_compact - compacts RFM file by removing holes.
random_create - creates a new RFM file with empty root block.
random_edit - a test program in /sys/random_edit that can do crude
              editing functions on a RFM file.  It is used to debug
              RFM and can also be used as an off-line editor for
              Scepter files.  (/data/scepter/edescrp).
random_file - a series of subs linked into the parent program
random_msgs.c - main program 
random_open - open an RFM file and check consistency
random_read - read data from an RFM file
random_write - write data to an RFM file
See the file random_msgs.h for information on the calling sequence to
RFM

              editing functions on a RFM file.  It is used to debug
              RFM and can also be used as an off-line editor for
              Scepter files.  (/data/scepter/edescrp).
random_file - a series of subs linked into the parent program
random_msgs.c - main program 
random_open - open an RFM file and check consistency
random_read - read data from an RFM file
random_write - write data to an RFM file
See the file random_msg
/*  random_compact - Compact a random file.
  84/04/22.     A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
random_compact(indexfp)
/* close file, compact, and reopen with same fp. */
word indexfp;
{
 char filename[BUFLEN], newname[BUFLEN];
 register struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
 struct rb_root_block  root_block;
 struct n_node   node;
 char *data;
 FILE *fp, *newfp;
 longword filesize;
 if ((filedesc = get_rf_index(indexfp)) == NULL_RANDOM_FILE)
  return;
 strcpy(filename, filedesc->rf_name);
 random_close(indexfp);
 tsprintf(newname, "tmp%d", abs(getpid()));
 if ((fp = fopen(filename, "rv")) == 0) /* if cannot */
  crash("fatal:random_compact - cannot reopen %s.\n", filename);
 fdelete(newname);  /* erase any old version of file */
 if ((newfp = fopen(newname, "rwv")) == 0)
  crash("fatal:random_compact - cannot open %s.\n", newname);
 fget(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), fp);
 if (root_block.rb_checkword != N_CHECK)  
  crash("fatal:random_compact - bad checkword on %s.\n", filename);
 fput(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), newfp);
 filesize = sizeof(root_block);
 tfprintf(stdout,"Active nodes: ");
 while (fget(&node, NODE_SIZE, fp) == NODE_SIZE) {
  if (node.n_checkword != N_CHECK) { /* if bad checkword */
   errlog("warn:random_compact - bad checkword at %d in %s.\n",
    (word) filesize, filename);
   continue;
  }
  tfprintf(stdout, "%d ",node.n_index);
  if (node.n_allocflag >= 0) /*if not allocated */
   fseek(fp, (longword) (node.n_size - NODE_SIZE), 1); /* skip */
  else { /* copy and compact */
   data = alloc(node.n_size - NODE_SIZE);
   fget(data, node.n_size - NODE_SIZE, fp);
   if ((node.n_size - node.n_data_size - NODE_SIZE) * 4 > 
     node.n_size) /* if more than 25 percent empty */
    node.n_size = node.n_data_size + NODE_SIZE +
     (node.n_data_size + NODE_SIZE) / 4;
    /* round down node to 25 percent empty */
   fput(&node, NODE_SIZE, newfp);
   fput(data, node.n_size - NODE_SIZE, newfp);
   free(data);
   filesize += node.n_size;
  }
 }
 rewind(newfp); 
 root_block.rb_filesize = filesize;
 fput(&root_block, sizeof(root_block), newfp);
 fclose(fp);
 fclose(newfp);
 call("/cmds/copy", newname, filename);
 fdelete(newname);
 random_fpopen(filedesc, filename); 
 tfprintf(stdout, "\n");
}

     (node.n_data_size + NODE_SIZE) / 4;

   fput(&node, NODE_SIZE, newfp);
   fput(data, node.n_size - NODE_SIZE, newfp);
   free(data);
   filesize += node.n_size;
  }

/*  random_close - Close a random file.
  84/04/22.     A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
random_close(fp)
word fp;
{
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
 if ((filedesc = get_rf_index(fp)) == NULL_RANDOM_FILE) 
  return; /* if bad index */
 random_fpclose(filedesc);
}
random_fpclose(filedesc)
/* close a random file, free all its links, and zero the entry */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
{
 struct  np_node_pointer *node_pointer, *tmp_node_pointer;
 struct  cp_cache_pointer *tmp_cache_pointer;
 fclose(filedesc->rf_fp);
 
 node_pointer = filedesc->rf_AV;
 /* free memory of free-node list */
 while (node_pointer->np_next != filedesc->rf_AV) {
  tmp_node_pointer = node_pointer->np_next;  
  node_pointer->np_next = node_pointer->np_next->np_next; 
  free(tmp_node_pointer);
 }
 free(filedesc->rf_AV);
{   /* free memory of hash table lists */
 register i;
 for (i=0; i < HASHSIZE; ++i) 
  while (filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i] != NULL_NODE_POINTER) {
   tmp_node_pointer = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i];
   filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i] = filedesc->rf_hashtbl[i]->np_next;
   free(tmp_node_pointer);
  }
 for (i=0; i < CACHESIZE; ++i)
  while(filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] != NULL_CACHE_POINTER) {
   tmp_cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i];
   filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i]->cp_next;
   free(tmp_cache_pointer->cp_data);
   free(tmp_cache_pointer);
  }
}
 zero (filedesc, sizeof(struct rf_random_file));
}

/*  random_msgs - Random file message handler
  84/04/28.   A. E. Klietz.
 
       Copyright (c) 1984, Alan E. Klietz.  All Rights reserved.
 
 This is the main loop that receives messages and executes
the proper routine in response. */
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
#include "random_msgs.h"
main()
{
 unsigned_word tid;
 word   size;
 struct ra_callcomplete callcomplete;
    struct ra_generic_msg  generic_msg;
 struct ra_generic_msg *reply_msg;
 char   *data;
 setup("random_file");
 for (;;) { /* loop forever */
  breakable();
  if ((tid = receive(0, &generic_msg, sizeof(generic_msg))) == 0)
   /* if received failed */
   crash("fatal:random_msgs - bad receive.\n");
  unbreakable();
  
  if (generic_msg.ra_checkword != N_CHECK) {
   errlog("warn:random_msgs - bad checkword.\n");
   continue;
  }
  switch (generic_msg.ra_msgnum) {
case RA_READMSG:
 size = random_read(generic_msg.ra_fp, generic_msg.ra_index,   
                 &data, generic_msg.ra_maxsize);
 if (size > MAX_XFER_SIZE) { /* if too big for without warning */
  reply_msg = alloc(sizeof(struct ra_readsize_reply));
  reply_msg->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
  reply_msg->ra_msgnum = RA_READSIZEMSG;
  reply_msg->ra_readsize = size;
 
  reply(tid, reply_msg, sizeof(struct ra_readsize_reply));
  free(reply_msg);
  if ((tid = receive(tid, &generic_msg, sizeof(generic_msg))) == 0) 
   crash("fatal:random_msgs - bad readproceed receive.\n");
  
  if (generic_msg.ra_checkword != N_CHECK || generic_msg.ra_msgnum != 
    RA_PROCEEDMSG) {
   errlog("warn:random_msgs - bad readproceed.\n");
   continue;
  }
 }
 if (size == 0) /* if no data allocated */
  data = alloc(sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
 data->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 data->ra_msgnum = RA_READMSG;
 data->ra_readsize = size;
 reply(tid, data, sizeof(struct ra_readsize_reply) + size);
 free(data);
 break;
                 
case RA_WRITESIZEMSG: /* upcoming write */
 reply_msg = alloc(sizeof(struct ra_proceed));
 reply_msg->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 reply_msg->ra_msgnum = RA_PROCEEDMSG;
 reply(tid, reply_msg, sizeof(struct ra_proceed));
 free(reply_msg);
 data = alloc(generic_msg.ra_writesize + sizeof(struct ra_writemsg));
 if ((tid = receive(tid, data, generic_msg.ra_writesize +
   sizeof(struct ra_writemsg))) == 0) 
  crash("fatal:random_msgs - bad write receive.\n");
 
 size = random_write(data->ra_fp, data->ra_index, data->ra_write_data,
   data->ra_data_writesize);
  
 free(data);
 reply_msg = alloc(sizeof(struct ra_writemsg_reply));
 reply_msg->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 reply_msg->ra_msgnum = RA_WRITEMSG;
 reply_msg->ra_writesize = size;
 reply(tid, reply_msg, sizeof(struct ra_writemsg_reply));
 free(reply_msg);
 break;
case RA_WRITEMSG: /* write */
 
 size = random_write(generic_msg.ra_fp, generic_msg.ra_index,
   generic_msg.ra_write_data, generic_msg.ra_data_writesize);
  
 reply_msg = alloc(sizeof(struct ra_writemsg_reply));
 reply_msg->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 reply_msg->ra_msgnum = RA_WRITEMSG;
 reply_msg->ra_writesize = size;
 reply(tid, reply_msg, sizeof(struct ra_writemsg_reply));
 free(reply_msg);
 break;
case RA_OPENMSG: /* open */
 callcomplete.ra_complete_code = random_open(generic_msg.ra_filename);
 reply(tid, &callcomplete, sizeof(callcomplete));
 break;
case RA_CREATEMSG: /* create */
 callcomplete.ra_complete_code = random_create(generic_msg.ra_filename,
              generic_msg.ra_initsize,
                  generic_msg.ra_cachesize);
 reply(tid, &callcomplete, sizeof(callcomplete));
 break;
case RA_CLOSEMSG: /* close */
 random_close(generic_msg.ra_fp);
 callcomplete.ra_complete_code = TRUE; /* always */
 reply(tid, &callcomplete, sizeof(callcomplete));
 break;
case RA_COMPACTMSG: /* compact */
 random_compact(generic_msg.ra_fp);
 callcomplete.ra_complete_code = TRUE; /* always */
 reply(tid, &callcomplete, sizeof(callcomplete));
 break;
  } /* end switch */
 } /* end for */
} /* end main */
               generic_msg.ra_cachesize);
 reply(tid, &callcomplete, sizeof(callcomplete));
 break;

 random_close(generic_

/*  random_file - Random file manager.
  84/05/04.   A. E. Klietz.
 
    Copyright (c) 1984,  Alan E. Klietz.   All Rights reserved.
 These are of set of routines to build and send messages to
the random file subtask (random_msgs) to manipulate random files.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
#include "random_msgs.h"
static unsigned_word rfm_tid; /* Tid if child rfm task */
char *bad_send = "fatal:rfm - unable to send to random_msgs";
FILE *random_open(filename)
char *filename;
{
 struct ra_openmsg openmsg;
 struct ra_callcomplete callcomplete;
 check_tid(); /* spawn random_msgs if necessary */
 openmsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 openmsg.ra_msgnum = RA_OPENMSG;
 strcpy(openmsg.ra_filename, filename);
 
 if (send(rfm_tid, &openmsg, &callcomplete, sizeof(openmsg)) == 0)   
  crash(bad_send);
 return(callcomplete.ra_complete_code);
}
FILE *random_create(filename, initsize, cachesize)
char *filename;
unsigned_word initsize, cachesize;
{
 struct ra_createmsg createmsg;
 struct ra_callcomplete callcomplete;
 check_tid(); /* spawn random_msgs if necessary */
 createmsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 createmsg.ra_msgnum = RA_CREATEMSG;
 strcpy(createmsg.ra_filename, filename);
 createmsg.ra_initsize = initsize;
 createmsg.ra_cachesize = cachesize;
 
 if (send(rfm_tid, &createmsg, &callcomplete, sizeof(createmsg)) == 0)
  crash(bad_send);
 return(callcomplete.ra_complete_code);
}
random_close(fp)
FILE *fp;
{
 struct ra_closemsg  closemsg;
 struct ra_callcomplete callcomplete;
 closemsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 closemsg.ra_msgnum = RA_CLOSEMSG;
 closemsg.ra_fp = fp;
 if (send(rfm_tid, &closemsg, &callcomplete, sizeof(closemsg)) == 0)
  crash(bad_send);
 return;
}
random_compact(fp)
FILE  *fp;
{
 struct ra_compactmsg compactmsg;
 struct ra_callcomplete callcomplete;
 compactmsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 compactmsg.ra_msgnum = RA_COMPACTMSG;
 compactmsg.ra_fp = fp;
 if (send(rfm_tid, &compactmsg, &callcomplete, sizeof(compactmsg)) == 0)
  crash(bad_send);
 return;
}
unsigned_word random_read(fp, index, data, maxsize)
FILE *fp;
word index;
char *data;
unsigned_word maxsize;
{
 extern unsigned_word random_generic_read();
 return(random_generic_read(fp, index, &data, maxsize, FALSE));
}
unsigned_word random_allocread(fp, index, data, maxsize)
FILE *fp;
word index;
char **data; /* pointer to data pointer */
unsigned_word maxsize;
{
 extern unsigned_word random_generic_read();
 return(random_generic_read(fp, index, data, maxsize, TRUE));
}
unsigned_word random_generic_read(fp, index, data, maxsize, ALLOCFLAG)
FILE *fp;
word index;
char **data;
unsigned_word maxsize;
boolean ALLOCFLAG;
{
 struct ra_readmsg  readmsg;
 struct ra_generic_msg generic_reply; 
 struct ra_generic_msg *reply_buffer;
 struct ra_proceed  proceed;
 unsigned_word finalsize;
 readmsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 readmsg.ra_msgnum = RA_READMSG;
 readmsg.ra_index = index;
 readmsg.ra_fp = fp;
 readmsg.ra_maxsize = maxsize;
 if (send(rfm_tid, &readmsg, &generic_reply, sizeof(readmsg)) == 0)
  crash(bad_send);
 if (generic_reply.ra_msgnum == RA_READSIZEMSG) {
  reply_buffer = alloc(generic_reply.ra_readsize + 
      sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
  proceed.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
  proceed.ra_msgnum = RA_PROCEEDMSG;
  if (send(rfm_tid, &proceed, reply_buffer, sizeof(proceed)) == 0)
   crash(bad_send);
  
  finalsize = reply_buffer->ra_readsize;
  if (ALLOCFLAG) 
   *data = alloc(finalsize);
  copy(*data, reply_buffer->ra_read_data, finalsize);
  free(reply_buffer);
  return(finalsize);
 }
 else {
  if (ALLOCFLAG) 
   if (generic_reply.ra_readsize > 0)
    *data = alloc(generic_reply.ra_readsize);
   else
    *data = NULL;
  if (generic_reply.ra_readsize > 0)
   copy(*data, generic_reply.ra_read_data, generic_reply.ra_readsize);
  return(generic_reply.ra_readsize);
 }
}
unsigned_word random_write(fp, index, data, size)
FILE *fp;
word index;
char *data;
unsigned_word size;
{
 struct ra_writemsg   *writemsg;
 struct ra_writesizemsg  writesizemsg;
 struct ra_writemsg_reply writemsg_reply;
 struct ra_proceed   proceed;
 if (size > MAX_XFER_SIZE) { /* if too big for without warning */
  writesizemsg.ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
  writesizemsg.ra_msgnum = RA_WRITESIZEMSG;
  writesizemsg.ra_writesize = size;
  if (send(rfm_tid, &writesizemsg, &proceed, sizeof(writesizemsg))
    == 0)
   crash(bad_send);
 }
 writemsg = alloc(size + sizeof(struct ra_writemsg));
 writemsg->ra_checkword = N_CHECK;
 writemsg->ra_msgnum = RA_WRITEMSG;
 writemsg->ra_index = index;
 writemsg->ra_fp = fp;
 writemsg->ra_data_writesize = size;
    copy(writemsg->ra_write_data, data, size);
 if (send(rfm_tid, writemsg, &writemsg_reply, size +
   sizeof(struct ra_writemsg)) == 0)
  crash(bad_send);
 free(writemsg);
 return(writemsg_reply.ra_writesize);
} 
check_tid()
{
 if (rfm_tid == 0) /* if not initialized */
  if ((rfm_tid = spawn("/std/random_msgs", 0, 0)) == 0)
   crash("fatal:rfm - unable to call random_msgs.\n");
}
writemsg->ra_msgnum = RA_WRITEMSG;
 writemsg->ra_index = index;
 writemsg->ra_fp = fp;
 writemsg->ra_data_writesize = size;

/*  random_cache - routines for reading/writing the
        in-memory cache.
  84/04/22.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
#include "random_msgs.h"
#define  MAX_ALLOCS    50 
/* Number of allocations before cleaning out the cache tables. 
 *
 * The cache tables must be periodically cleaned out by freeing
 * all the allocated memory.  Otherwise the memory fills with
 * tiny holes and huge amounts of memory get wasted.
 */
static unsigned alloc_count;
word read_cache(filedesc, index, maxsize, data)
/* check the cache and read in the data if possible.
KLUDGE:
   'data' is allocated to necessary size 
 Reserve top few words for ra_readmsg_reply information */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word   index;
unsigned_word   maxsize;
char     **data; /* pointer to data pointer */
{
 register struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 word size;
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0)
  return(0); /* if no cache */
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
    cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash];
 while (cache_pointer != NULL_CACHE_POINTER &&
   cache_pointer->cp_index != index)
  cache_pointer = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 if (cache_pointer == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) /* if not found */
  return(0);
 if ((size = min(maxsize, cache_pointer->cp_sizedata)) == 0)
  return(0); /* if no data */
 *data = alloc(size + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
 copy(*data + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply), cache_pointer->cp_data, size);
 return(size);
}
 
remove_cache(filedesc, index)
/* remove the given item from the cache */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word index;
{
 register struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer, *tmp_cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 word size;
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0)
  return; /* if no cache */
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
    tmp_cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash];
 if (tmp_cache_pointer == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) 
  return;
 if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_index == index) { 
  cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer;
  filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash] = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 }
 else { 
  while (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next != NULL_CACHE_POINTER &&
     tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next->cp_index != index)
    tmp_cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
  if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) 
   return;
  cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
  tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 }
 filedesc->rf_cachesize -= cache_pointer->cp_sizedata;
 free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
 free(cache_pointer);
}
add_cache(filedesc, index, size, data)
/* Add a new node to the cache (if it fits).  Delete an old item using
   an end-around last-in-bucket search if needed to make room in the cache */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word   index;
unsigned_word   size;
char     *data;
{
 register struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer, *tmp_cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 if (++alloc_count > MAX_ALLOCS) { /* If memory is getting chaotic */
  empty_caches();  /* Clean out all caches and start fresh */
  alloc_count = 0;
 }
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0 || size == 0)
  return; /* if no cache, or no data */
 if (size <= 0 || size > filedesc->rf_maxcachesize / 4)
  return; /* if too big for cache */
 while (size + filedesc->rf_cachesize > filedesc->rf_maxcachesize) {
  /* go end-around and remove the bottom node from the bucket */
   register i;
  i = filedesc->rf_cache_nextremove;
  while (filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] == NULL_CACHE_POINTER)
   i = (i == 0 ? CACHESIZE - 1 : i-1);
  tmp_cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i];
  if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) {
   cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer;
   filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] = NULL_CACHE_POINTER;
  }
  else {
   while(tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next->cp_next != NULL_CACHE_POINTER)
    tmp_cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
   cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
   tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next = NULL_CACHE_POINTER;  
  }
  filedesc->rf_cache_nextremove = (i == 0 ? CACHESIZE - 1 : i-1);
  filedesc->rf_cachesize -= cache_pointer->cp_sizedata;
  free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
  free(cache_pointer);
    }
    /* now add new cache entry to table */
 
 cache_pointer = alloc(sizeof(struct cp_cache_pointer));
 cache_pointer->cp_index = index;
 cache_pointer->cp_sizedata = size;
 cache_pointer->cp_data = alloc(size);
 copy(cache_pointer->cp_data, data, size);
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
 cache_pointer->cp_next = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash]; 
 filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash] = cache_pointer;
 filedesc->rf_cachesize += size;
}

/*  random_file.h - random_file definitions.
  84/04/14.  A. E. Klietz
*/
#define  N_CHECK   0xD1A5  /* header of node */
#define  MAXFILES  5    /* Max number of open random files */
#define  HASHSIZE  61   /* Size of hash table (prime #) */
#define  CACHESIZE  17   /* Size of cache table (prime #) */
#define  SIZE_CHUNK  512   /* Size of chunk to increase file space */
#define  NUM_CHUNKS  32   /* Number of chunks to increase each
             time file space runs out */
/* Definition for a node in a random_file file */
struct n_node {
 unsigned_word n_checkword;  /* start of new node */
 word   n_allocflag;  /* < 0 if allocated. */
 unsigned_word n_index;   /* index of node */
 unsigned_word n_size;    /* size of node. */
 unsigned_word n_data_size;  /* size of data in node */
 char   n_data[0];   /* start of data for node. */
}; 
#define  NULL_NODE ((struct n_node *) 0)
#define  NODE_SIZE sizeof(struct n_node)
#define  N_MAXSIZE   16384  /* Maximum size of node = SIZE_CHUNK * NUM_CHUNKS */
#define  N_MINSIZE 500    /* Minimum number of unused bytes before the node
          gets split apart. */
/* Definition of a node pointer */
struct np_node_pointer {
 struct np_node_pointer *np_next; /* link to next node */
 longword    np_offset; /* offset of node into file */
 union {
  unsigned_word  np_sizenode;  /* size of free node */
  unsigned_word  np_index;   /* index of allocated node */
 } np_data;
};
#define NULL_NODE_POINTER  ((struct np_node_pointer *) 0)
/* Definition of a cache pointer */
struct cp_cache_pointer {
 struct cp_cache_pointer *cp_next; /* link to next node */
 unsigned_word   cp_index; /* index of cached node */
 unsigned_word   cp_sizedata; /* size of data in node */
 char     *cp_data; /* data inside cached node */
};
#define NULL_CACHE_POINTER ((struct cp_cache_pointer *) 0)
/* Definition of a random file descriptor */
struct rf_random_file {
 FILE     *rf_fp;  /* File (0 if closed) */
 struct np_node_pointer *rf_AV;  /* Head of available space list */  
 struct np_node_pointer *rf_hashtbl[HASHSIZE]; /* table of allocated nodes */
 char     rf_name[BUFLEN]; /* name of file */
 struct np_node_pointer *rf_allocp; /* current position of end-round ptr */
 unsigned_word   rf_maxcachesize; /* limit of data in cache */
 unsigned_word   rf_cachesize; /* current size of cache */
 struct cp_cache_pointer *rf_cachetbl[CACHESIZE]; /* cache table */
 word     rf_cache_nextremove; /* end-around cache removal ptr */
};
#define NULL_RANDOM_FILE ((struct rf_random_file *) 0)
/* Definition of the root block */
struct rb_root_block {
 unsigned_word   rb_corrupt;  /* if nonzero, then file is corrupt */
 unsigned_word   rb_checkword; /* Identify file as a random file */
 unsigned_word   rb_maxcachesize; /* size of cache in bytes */
 longword    rb_filesize; /* size of file in bytes */
 struct n_node     rb_AVnode;  /* Head of available node list */
 byte     rb_dummy[480 - sizeof(rb_corrupt) - 
        sizeof(rb_checkword) - sizeof(rb_filesize)
        - sizeof(rb_maxcachesize) - sizeof(rb_AVnode)];
};
extern struct rf_random_file filelist[MAXFILES];
/* Master file list of random files. */
/*
  random_read(fp, word index, char *data, word maxsize)
   read data from file fp at index.  A maximum of
maxsize words are returned.  The number of words read is returned. 
  random_write(fp, word index, char *data, word size)
   write data to file fp at index.  The number of words
written is returned.
   fp = random_open(filename)
   Open a random file.  Return zero if unable to open   
file.  
   fp = random_create(filename, initsize, cachesize)
   Create and open file with an initial length of 'initsize'
bytes.  Size must be at least 1024.  'cachesize' is size of cache
(up to 16384 bytes).  Zero is returned if file cannot be created. 
  random_close(fp)
   Close the given random file
  random_compact(fp)
   Request a compaction of file fp.  This could take 
over a minute for large files.
*/
  fp = random_open(filename)
   Open a random file.  Return zero if unab

/*  Random_msgs.h - Random file message definitions
  84/04/28. A. E. Klietz.
(hint for AEK: use varying length strings for object/monster names
changed scepter constants  - max objs per room - 25
        rooms per cluster -  5)
*/
#define  MAX_XFER_SIZE 256  /* size of smallest xfer w/o size reply */
#define  PARAM_SIZE   20  /* size of parameters other than databuf */
enum ra_msg_type {RA_READMSG, RA_READSIZEMSG, RA_WRITEMSG, RA_WRITESIZEMSG,
 RA_PROCEEDMSG, RA_OPENMSG, RA_CREATEMSG, RA_CLOSEMSG, RA_COMPACTMSG};
struct  ra_generic_msg {
 word ra_checkword;       /* N_CHECK checkword */
 word ra_msgnum;          /* message number (read, write, etc) */
 byte ra_dummy[PARAM_SIZE]; /* other misc parameters */
 char ra_databuf[MAX_XFER_SIZE];
};
struct ra_proceed {          /* Proceed with data transfer */
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
};
struct ra_readmsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_index;       /* Index of record to read */
 word ra_fp;          /* File pointer of file to read */
 word ra_maxsize;     /* Max size of record to read */
};
struct ra_readsize_reply { /* Size of upcoming read reply */
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_readsize;
};
struct ra_readmsg_reply {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_readsize;
 char ra_read_data[0];
};
struct ra_writesizemsg { /* Size of upcoming write */
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_writesize;
};
struct ra_writemsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_index;  /* Index of record to write */
 word ra_fp;          /* file pointer of file to write */ 
 word ra_data_writesize;   /* Size of data to write (up to 512) */
 word ra_write_data[0];
};
struct ra_writemsg_reply {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_writesize; /* Size of data actually written. 0 if error. */
};
struct ra_callcomplete {
 word ra_complete_code; /* returned value from open/close/compact/create */
};
struct ra_openmsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 char ra_filename[BUFLEN];
};
struct ra_createmsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 char ra_filename[BUFLEN];
 word ra_initsize;
 word ra_cachesize;
};
struct ra_closemsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_cdummy;
 word ra_fp;
};
struct ra_compactmsg {
 word ra_checkword;
 word ra_msgnum;
 word ra_cdummy;
 word ra_fp;
};
/*
  random_read(fp, word index, char *data, word maxsize)
   read data from file fp at index.  A maximum of
maxsize words are returned.  The number of words read is returned. 
  random_write(fp, word index, char *data, word size)
   write data to file fp at index.  The number of words
written is returned.
   fp = random_open(filename)
   Open a random file.  Return zero if unable to open   
file.  
   fp = random_create(filename, initsize, cachesize)
   Create and open file with an initial length of 'initsize'
bytes.  Size must be at least 1024.  'cachesize' is size of cache
(up to 16384 bytes).  Zero is returned if file cannot be created. 
  random_close(fp)
   Close the given random file
  random_compact(fp)
   Request a compaction of file fp.  This could take 
over a minute for large files.
*/
  fp = random_open(filename)
   Open a random file.  Return zero if unable to open   
file.  
   fp = random_create(filename, initsize, cachesize)
   Create and open file with an initial length of 'initsize'
bytes.  Size must be at least 1024.  'cachesize' is size of cache
(up to 16384 bytes).  Zero is returned if file cannot be created. 
  random_close(fp)
   Close the given random file

/*  update - update the room files.
  84/06/21. A. E. Klietz.
*/
#define NONSTANDARD
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "/def/multidef.h"
#include "^^def/scepter.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#define TASKCLOSED_TXT 200
#define NUMRUN_TXT 204
#define MSPEED_TXT 206
/* The following definitions are duplicated in buy.c and whydie.c */
#define NUM_OBITS   3   /* Number of obituaries in the paper */
#define OLD_OBIT 251 /* Base index for yesterday's obits */
#define NEW_OBIT 261 /* Base index for today's obits */
#define MONTHDAY 259 /* Day-of-month for yesterday's obits */
int  lencounter, objlistlen;
int  (*encounterindex)[6];
int  (*objindex)[6];
word obit_lvl[NUM_OBITS];
word mspeed;
extern word slottbl[MAX_PORTS + 1];
extern char taskclosed;
extern word numrun;
extern TI_TIME lastupdate;
extern unsigned elist;
static word monthday;
update()
{
 register i;
 char buf[BUFLEN];
 for (i=0; i < MAX_PORTS + 1; ++i)
  if (slottbl[i] > -1)
   writeseg(i, slottbl[i], FALSE);
 tsprintf(buf, "%c", taskclosed);
 writedesc(TASKCLOSED_TXT, buf);
 tsprintf(buf, "%d", mspeed);
 writedesc(MSPEED_TXT, buf);
 random_write(elist, 1, encounterindex, lencounter * 6 * sizeof(word));
 random_write(elist, 2, objindex, objlistlen * 6 * sizeof(word));
}
clean_update()
/* Update and free EVERYTHING to clean up holes in memory. */
{
 register i,j;
 char buf[BUFLEN], nametable[MAX_PORTS][12];
 unsigned trmtable[MAX_PORTS], suspended_trm;
 register struct us_user  *tmpuser;
 i = -1;
 suspended_trm = user->us_trm;
 user = usertail;
 while (user != NULL) {
  if (user->us_status != SNORMAL) {
   user = user->us_nextuser;
   continue;
  }
  ++i;
  My_mask = 1 << user->us_trm;
  user->us_usweap = NULL;
  stopusing(user, user->us_usarm);
  stopusing(user, user->us_usshield);
  tmpuser = user->us_nextuser;
  deleteuser(user, usertail);
  strncpy(nametable[i], user->us_name, 12);
  trmtable[i] = user->us_trm;
  if (writeplayer(user, user->us_name, genuser[user->us_trm].gn_userindex, FALSE))
   writeusr(user);
  else
   --i;
  user = tmpuser;
 }
 for (j=0; j < MAX_PORTS + 1; ++j)
  if (slottbl[j] > -1) {
   writeseg(j, slottbl[j], TRUE);
   slottbl[j] = -1;
  }
/* Everything is released.  Now read it all back in. */
 for (j=0; j <= i; ++j) {
  user = alloc(sizeof(struct us_user));
  user->us_checkword = CHECKWORD;
  readplayer(user, nametable[j], genuser[trmtable[j]].gn_userindex);
        if (user->us_name[0] == '\0') {
   error_handler(0);
   crash(desc(543));
   /* Failed to read back in user from update!\n */
  }
  user->us_trm = trmtable[j];
  user->us_entry = XCMD;
  user->us_status = SNORMAL;
  user->us_nextuser = usertail;
  usertail = user;
  placeplayer(user, user->us_rmcode);
 }
  
 user = usertail;
 while (user != NULL)
  if (user->us_trm == suspended_trm)
   break;
  else
   user = user->us_nextuser;
 if (user == NULL)
  crash(545);
  /* Sce545 - Could not restart suspended user!\n */
 My_mask = 1 << user->us_trm;
 tsprintf(buf, "%c", taskclosed);
 writedesc(TASKCLOSED_TXT, buf);
 tsprintf(buf, "%d", mspeed);
 writedesc(MSPEED_TXT, buf);
 random_write(elist, 1, encounterindex, lencounter * 6 * sizeof(word));
 random_write(elist, 2, objindex, objlistlen * 6 * sizeof(word));
}
break_trap()
{
 unbreakable();
 crash("Sce000 - Break signal received.\n");
}
error_trap()
/* Called on error.   Return 0 to abort, else error recovered. */
{
 char buf[BUFLEN];
 error_handler(0);  /* Turn off further error handling */
 update();
 tsprintf(buf, "%d", numrun+1);
 writedesc(NUMRUN_TXT, buf);
 tsprintf(buf, "%d", monthday);
 writedesc(MONTHDAY, buf);
 if (user != NULL && user->us_checkword == CHECKWORD) 
  errlog("Player %s caused the pta with the \"%s\" cmd in room %d.\n",
   user->us_name, user->us_lastcmd, w(user->us_rmcode));
 return(0);  /* Crash */
}
do_initialize()
{
 register i;
 struct  bt_broken_time *broken_time;
 char  *string;
 if ((elist = random_open("/data/scepter/elist")) == 0)
  crash(desc(1252));
  /* Sce1252 - Unable to open /data/scepter/elist!\n */
 if ((lencounter = random_allocread(elist, 1, &encounterindex, 6000))
   == 0) {
  lencounter = 1;
  encounterindex = alloc(sizeof(int) * 6);
 }
 else
  lencounter /= sizeof(int) * 6;
 if ((objlistlen = random_allocread(elist, 2, &objindex, 6000)) == 0) {
  objlistlen = 1;
  objindex = alloc(sizeof(int) * 6);
 }
 else
  objlistlen /= sizeof(int) * 6;
 
 for (i=0; i < MAX_PORTS + 1; ++i)
  slottbl[i] = -1;
 tsscanf(desc(MONTHDAY), "%d", &monthday); 
 tsscanf(desc(TASKCLOSED_TXT), "%c", &taskclosed);
 tsscanf(desc(NUMRUN_TXT), "%d", &numrun);
 mspeed = 6; /* default */
 tsscanf(desc(MSPEED_TXT), "%d", &mspeed);
 time(&lastupdate);
 broken_time = bt_time(&lastupdate);
 if (monthday != broken_time->bt_day) { /* If new day */
  monthday = broken_time->bt_day;
  for (i=0; i < NUM_OBITS; ++i) {
   string = desc(i + NEW_OBIT);
   writedesc(i + OLD_OBIT, string);
  }
 }
 else 
  for (i=0; i < NUM_OBITS; ++i) {
   string = desc(i + NEW_OBIT);
   tsscanf(string, "%d", &obit_lvl[i]);
  }
 error_handler(error_trap);
 exc_handler(EXC_BREAK, 0, break_trap);
}

ches()

  filedesc = &filelist[j];

   continue;

   if ((cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i]) == NULL_CACHE_POINTER)
    continue;
   do {
    tmp_cache_pointer = cache_pointer->cp_next;
    free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
    free(cache_pointer);
    cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer;
         } while (cache_pointer != NULL_CACHE_POINTER);
  }

    tmp_cache_pointer = cache_pointer->cp_next;
    free(cache_poin
/*  random_cache - routines for reading/writing the
        in-memory cache.
  84/04/22.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "random_file.h"
#include "random_msgs.h"
word read_cache(filedesc, index, maxsize, data)
/* check the cache and read in the data if possible.
KLUDGE:
   'data' is allocated to necessary size 
 Reserve top few words for ra_readmsg_reply information */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word   index;
unsigned_word   maxsize;
char     **data; /* pointer to data pointer */
{
 register struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 word size;
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0)
  return(0); /* if no cache */
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
    cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash];
 while (cache_pointer != NULL_CACHE_POINTER &&
   cache_pointer->cp_index != index)
  cache_pointer = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 if (cache_pointer == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) /* if not found */
  return(0);
 if ((size = min(maxsize, cache_pointer->cp_sizedata)) == 0)
  return(0); /* if no data */
 *data = alloc(size + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply));
 copy(*data + sizeof(struct ra_readmsg_reply), cache_pointer->cp_data, size);
 return(size);
}
 
remove_cache(filedesc, index)
/* remove the given item from the cache */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word index;
{
 register struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer, *tmp_cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 word size;
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0)
  return; /* if no cache */
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
    tmp_cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash];
 if (tmp_cache_pointer == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) 
  return;
 if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_index == index) { 
  cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer;
  filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash] = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 }
 else { 
  while (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next != NULL_CACHE_POINTER &&
     tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next->cp_index != index)
    tmp_cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
  if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) 
   return;
  cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
  tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next = cache_pointer->cp_next;
 }
 filedesc->rf_cachesize -= cache_pointer->cp_sizedata;
 free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
 free(cache_pointer);
}
add_cache(filedesc, index, size, data)
/* Add a new node to the cache (if it fits).  Delete an old item using
   an end-around last-in-bucket search if needed to make room in the cache */
struct rf_random_file *filedesc;
unsigned_word   index;
unsigned_word   size;
char     *data;
{
 struct cp_cache_pointer *cache_pointer, *tmp_cache_pointer;
    word hash;
 if (filedesc->rf_maxcachesize == 0 || size == 0)
  return; /* if no cache, or no data */
 if (size <= 0 || size > filedesc->rf_maxcachesize / 4)
  return; /* if too big for cache */
 while (size + filedesc->rf_cachesize > filedesc->rf_maxcachesize) {
  /* go end-around and remove the bottom node from the bucket */
   register i;
  i = filedesc->rf_cache_nextremove;
  while (filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] == NULL_CACHE_POINTER)
   i = (i == 0 ? CACHESIZE - 1 : i-1);
  tmp_cache_pointer = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i];
  if (tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next == NULL_CACHE_POINTER) {
   cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer;
   filedesc->rf_cachetbl[i] = NULL_CACHE_POINTER;
  }
  else {
   while(tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next->cp_next != NULL_CACHE_POINTER)
    tmp_cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
   cache_pointer = tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next;
   tmp_cache_pointer->cp_next = NULL_CACHE_POINTER;  
  }
  filedesc->rf_cache_nextremove = (i == 0 ? CACHESIZE - 1 : i-1);
  filedesc->rf_cachesize -= cache_pointer->cp_sizedata;
  free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
  free(cache_pointer);
    }
    /* now add new cache entry to table */
 
 cache_pointer = alloc(sizeof(struct cp_cache_pointer));
 cache_pointer->cp_index = index;
 cache_pointer->cp_sizedata = size;
 cache_pointer->cp_data = alloc(size);
 copy(cache_pointer->cp_data, data, size);
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
 cache_pointer->cp_next = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash]; 
 filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash] = cache_pointer;
 filedesc->rf_cachesize += size;
}
ache_pointer->cp_sizedata;
  free(cache_pointer->cp_data);
  free(cache_pointer);
    }
    /* now add new cache entry to table */
 
 cache_pointer = alloc(sizeof(struct cp_cache_pointer));
 cache_pointer->cp_index = index;
 cache_pointer->cp_sizedata = size;
 cache_pointer->cp_data = alloc(size);
 copy(cache_pointer->cp_data, data, size);
 hash = abs(index % CACHESIZE);
 cache_pointer->cp_next = filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash]; 
 filedesc->rf_cachetbl[hash] = cache_pointer;
 fileda

  do {
   mwrite(My_mask, "  %s\n", printobj(object, FALSE));
   object = object->ob_next;

 }
}

boolean show_userfile(userindex)
unsigned  userindex;
{
 boolean  found = FALSE;
 struct ud_user_directory user_directory;
 register i;
 if (random_read(eperson, userindex << 3, &user_directory, 
   sizeof(user_directory)) < sizeof(user_directory)) 
  return(FALSE);
 
 for (i=0; i < MAX_ENTRIES; ++i)
  if (user_directory.ud_name[i][0] != '\0') {
   found = TRUE;
   mwrite(My_mask, "  %s\n", user_directory.ud_name[i]);
  }
 return(found);
}

=
/*   room.h - Scepter room definitions
   84/06/04.  A. E. Klietz.
*/
#include "/std/def.h"
#include "^^def/user.h"
#include "^^def/monster.h"
#include "^^def/object.h"
struct ro_room {
 word  ro_adjoin[7];  /* adjoining rooms 0-4095 */
 word  ro_desccode;  /* room description code 0-32767 */
 bitfield ro_whichencounter : 8; /* monster encounter type */ 
 bitfield ro_encountertime : 10; /* seconds between encounters */
 bitfield ro_notifydm : 1; /* TRUE if DM notified on entry */
 bitfield ro_safe : 1;  /* TRUE if room is safe haven */
 bitfield ro_lastdir : 4;  /* last known direction */
 struct us_user  *ro_rmplayertail; /* link to user list */
 struct mo_monster  *ro_rmmonstertail;  /* link to monster list */
 struct ob_objectr  *ro_rmobjecttail;   /* link to object list */
};
struct new_room {
 word  new_adjoin[7];  /* adjoining rooms 0-4095 */
 word  new_desccode;  /* room description code 0-32767 */
 bitfield new_whichencounter : 8; /* monster encounter type */ 
 bitfield    new_version : 2;        /* Room version code */
 bitfield new_encountertime : 10; /* seconds between encounters */
 bitfield new_notifydm : 1; /* TRUE if DM notified on entry */
 bitfield new_safe : 1;  /* TRUE if room is safe haven */
 bitfield new_lastdir : 4;  /* last known direction */
 struct us_user  *new_rmplayertail; /* link to user list */
 struct mo_monster  *new_rmmonstertail;  /* link to monster list */
 struct ob_objectr  *new_rmobjecttail;   /* link to object list */
};
struct ro_room  *room;
struct new_room *newroom;
main()
{
 room = alloc(sizeof(struct ro_room)); 
 newroom = alloc(sizeof(struct new_room));
 tfprintf(stdout, "%d, %d.\n", sizeof(struct ro_room), sizeof(struct new_room));
 room->ro_whichencounter = 170;
 room->ro_encountertime = 623;
 newroom->new_whichencounter = 170;
 newroom->new_encountertime = 623;
 tfprintf(stdout, "%d %d %d %d", room->new_whichencounter,
  room->new_encountertime, newroom->ro_whichencounter,
  newroom->ro_encountertime);
 tfprintf(stdout, "%d", newroom->new_version);
}
